        for c in e.after.keys() {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _backfill_absence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition='' AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,c])?;}
        let fill=next.backfills.iter().find(|f|f.key()==key).ok_or_else(||refusal("missing historical declaration"))?;
        let row_source=row_source(tx,t,&id,sources.get(&fill.table))?;
        for(c,kind)in &fill.inputs {
            // A generic id argument cannot erase the source column's lifetime.
            let kind=row_source.as_deref().and_then(|t|t.columns.get(c)).filter(|k|protected(k)).unwrap_or(kind);
            if let Ok(value)=e.inputs.field(c) {
                let binding=input_ref(&key,c);
                let unchanged=prior.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|p.inputs.field(c).ok()==Some(value));
                capture_input_refs(tx,t,&id,c,&binding,kind,value,unchanged)?;
            }
        }
    }
    drop(rows);drop(stmt);
    tx.conn().execute_batch("DROP TABLE temp._backfill_pending; DROP TABLE temp._backfill_originals;")?;
    // The source-index rebuild and every transformed dependency require full
    // candidate key/cap/maintain validation, even when its declaration is equal.
    tx.set_meta("activation_population","true")?;
    Ok(())
}
fn collect_reads(body:&[crate::ir::Stmt],out:&mut BTreeSet<(String,String)>){
    use crate::ir::{Expr,Stmt};
    fn expr(e:&Expr,out:&mut BTreeSet<(String,String)>){match e{
        Expr::Scan(r)=>{out.insert((r.table.clone(),r.index.clone()));for k in &r.prefix{if let Some(e)=k.scalar(){expr(e,out);}}if let Some(range)=&r.range {for e in [&range.gt,&range.gte,&range.lt,&range.lte].into_iter().flatten(){expr(e,out);}}if let Some(e)=&r.filter{expr(e,out);}},
        Expr::Get(r)|Expr::Exists(r)=>{out.insert((r.table.clone(),r.index.clone()));for e in &r.key{expr(e,out);}},
        Expr::Field{base,..}|Expr::Unary{value:base,..}=>expr(base,out),Expr::Array(es)|Expr::Builtin{args:es,..}=>{for e in es{expr(e,out);}},
        Expr::Object(fs)=>{for e in fs.values(){expr(e,out);}},Expr::Shape{base,fields}=>{expr(base,out);for e in fields.values(){expr(e,out);}},
        Expr::Binary{left,right,..}=>{expr(left,out);expr(right,out);},Expr::Block{body,value}=>{collect_reads(body,out);expr(value,out);},_=>()
    }}
    for s in body{match s{Stmt::Let{value,..}|Stmt::Const{value,..}|Stmt::Assign{value,..}|Stmt::Return(value)=>expr(value,out),Stmt::Require{condition,..}=>expr(condition,out),Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body}=>{expr(condition,out);collect_reads(then_body,out);collect_reads(else_body,out);},_=>()}}
}

// Prune forgets historical values, retaining only the fact that exact reversal
// is no longer available. No tombstone can be queried by an app.
pub(super) fn prune(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    if present(tx)? {tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_evidence.t AND r.id=_backfill_evidence.id); DELETE FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_pruned.t AND r.id=_backfill_pruned.id); INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_pruned(t,id,definition,active,retained_outputs) SELECT t,id,definition,active,retained_outputs FROM _backfill_evidence; DELETE FROM _backfill_evidence; DELETE FROM _backfill_absence WHERE (definition!='' AND definition NOT LIKE '$native:%') OR NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_absence.t AND r.id=_backfill_absence.id);")?;}
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn inventory_sql(conn:&Connection)->Result<&'static str,RuntimeError>{
    let exists:bool=conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_backfill_evidence')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    Ok(if exists {" UNION ALL SELECT c.name||'.backfill#'||substr(e.definition,1,12),'backfill',c.name,e.definition FROM _backfill_evidence e JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=e.t GROUP BY e.t,e.definition "}else{""})
}

pub(crate) fn has_evidence(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{present(tx)}
pub(super) fn columns(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema)->Result<BTreeMap<String,BTreeSet<String>>,RuntimeError>{
    let mut out:BTreeMap<String,BTreeSet<String>>=BTreeMap::new();
    for f in &next.backfills {out.entry(f.table.clone()).or_default().extend(f.outputs.iter().cloned());}
    if present(tx)? {
        let mut statement=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition FROM _backfill_definitions WHERE digest IN (SELECT definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=1 OR retained_outputs!='[]' UNION SELECT definition FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE active=1 OR retained_outputs!='[]')")?;
        for text in statement.query_map([],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))? {
            let f:Backfill=decode(&text?)?;out.entry(f.table).or_default().extend(f.outputs);
        }
    }
    Ok(out)
}
fn validate_lookup(tx:&Tx<'_>,ti:i64,prefix:usize,table:&str,index:&str)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT k,id FROM i WHERE ti=?1 ORDER BY k")?;let mut rows=stmt.query([ti])?;
    let mut prior=None;let mut first=String::new();let mut duplicate=false;let mut bad=super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
    while let Some(row)=rows.next()? {
        let key:Vec<u8>=row.get(0)?;let id:String=row.get(1)?;let mut group=crate::key::decode(&key).ok_or_else(||refusal("invalid historical lookup key"))?;group.truncate(prefix);
        if group.contains(&Value::Null){continue;}
        if prior.as_ref()==Some(&group) {
            if !duplicate {bad.add(&first,"duplicate historical lookup key");duplicate=true;}
            bad.add(&id,"duplicate historical lookup key");
        }else{prior=Some(group);first=id;duplicate=false;}
    }
    if bad.count>0{return Err(refusal(format!("historical lookup {table}.{index} is not unique: {}; prepare a unique bridge before deriving values",bad.describe())))}
    Ok(())
}

// Each original adoption cohort saw one snapshot. Re-entry restores that same
// view across all its declarations; later cohorts read earlier cohorts' outputs.
fn restore_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=(SELECT o.data FROM temp._backfill_originals o WHERE o.t=r.t AND o.id=r.id) WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM temp._backfill_originals o WHERE o.t=r.t AND o.id=r.id)",[])?;Ok(())
}
// A reversed, originally absent cell must have the same meaning in explicit
// arguments, point lookups and lookup indexes. Null is a temporary source-view
// marker; the saved physical image is restored before committing any patch.
fn mask_unprovided(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT r.t,r.id,octet_length(r.data) FROM r JOIN temp._backfill_unprovided m ON m.t=r.t AND m.id=r.id ORDER BY r.t,r.id")?;
    let mut rows=q.query([])?;
    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;
        let mut meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);meter.heap((r.get::<_,i64>(2)?.max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
        let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;
        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
        if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {
            let mut columns=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT column_name FROM temp._backfill_unprovided WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?;
            for c in columns.query_map(params![t,id],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))? {fields.entry(c?).or_insert(Value::Null);}
        }
        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
    }Ok(())
}
// Recorded later cohorts originally saw earlier releases' completed patches.
// Replaying those older cohorts virtually preserves that ordering; fresh
// declarations still run first against the untouched predecessor snapshot.
fn replay_earlier_cohorts(tx:&Tx<'_>,epoch:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,octet_length(patch),patch FROM temp._backfill_pending WHERE CAST(json_extract(evidence,'$.epoch') AS INTEGER)<?1 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
    let mut rows=stmt.query([epoch])?;
    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;
        let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
        let Some(bytes)=bytes else{continue};
        let mut admission=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);admission.heap((bytes.max(0) as u64).saturating_add(r.get::<_,i64>(2)?.max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
        let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;
        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
        let patch:BTreeMap<String,Value>=decode(&r.get::<_,String>(3)?)?;
        if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {fields.extend(patch);}
        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
    }Ok(())
}

fn historical_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>,epoch:i64,keys:&BTreeSet<String>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=1 AND epoch=?1 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
    let mut rows=q.query([epoch])?;
    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;let key:String=r.get(2)?;if !keys.contains(&key){continue;}
        let data:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
        let Some(data)=data else {continue};
        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
        let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;let e=evidence(tx,t,&id,&key)?.unwrap();
        let fields=match &mut row {Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f)=>f,_=>return Err(refusal("invalid historical row"))};
        if let Value::Row(inputs)|Value::Map(inputs)=&e.inputs {for(c,value)in inputs {
            if e.absent.contains(c) || !fields.contains_key(c) || fields.get(c)==Some(value) {
                let invalid:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,input_ref(&key,c)],|r|r.get(0))?;
                if invalid {return Err(refusal(format!("historical backfill reference {c} on {id} lost its parent lifetime")));}
                if e.absent.contains(c) {fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}else{fields.entry(c.clone()).or_insert(value.clone());}
            }
        }}
        for c in e.after.keys() {if !e.before.contains_key(c) {fields.insert(c.clone(),Value::Null);}}
        for(c,value)in e.before {fields.insert(c,value);}
        // Keep the receipt's omitted-input meaning separate from its later
        // rollback output preimage/default.
        for c in &e.absent {if let Ok(value)=e.inputs.field(c){fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}}
        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
    } Ok(())
}
fn rebuild_reads(tx:&Tx<'_>,source:&Schema,reads:&BTreeSet<(String,String)>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    for (name,index) in reads {
        let old=&source.tables[name].indexes[index];let ti=tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?;
        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[ti])?;
        super::activation::walk(tx,tx.num(name)?,|id,mut row|{
            materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,&source.tables[name]);
            for key in index_keys(old,&row,id){tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO i(ti,k,id) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![ti,key,id])?;}Ok(())
        })?;
        if old.unique {validate_lookup(tx,ti,old.components.len(),name,index)?;}
    } Ok(())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#6-bakeoff-repairs — computed values obey the ordinary text minimum/format law before narrowing.
fn validate_output(name:&str,table:&Table,column:&str,value:&Value)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    crate::engine::validate::validate_column(name,column,&table.columns[column],Some(value))?;
    for constraint in &table.constraints {if let crate::schema::Constraint::Text{field,min,format}=constraint {
        if field==column {if let Value::String(text)=value {crate::engine::validate::validate_text(&format!("{name}.{column}"),text,*min,*format)?;}}
    }}Ok(())
}

// Ordinary cell bindings can carry a newer transformed reference type than
// the archived whole-row definition. Copy those exact lifetime bindings before
// restoration consumes its ordinary ledger; same-value replay never rebinds.
fn capture_input_refs(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,column:&str,binding:&str,kind:&crate::schema::ColumnType,value:&Value,unchanged:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    if !unchanged {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,binding])?;}
    let mut admission=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);admission.heap(value.retained_heap_bytes().saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
    fn ids(value:&Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value {Value::Id(id)=>{out.insert(id.clone());},Value::Array(values)=>for value in values {ids(value,out)},_=>()}}
    let mut values=BTreeSet::new();ids(value,&mut values);let mut known=BTreeSet::new();
    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT parent_t,parent_id,invalid FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3")?;
    let mut rows=stmt.query(params![t,id,column])?;
    while let Some(row)=rows.next()? {
        let parent:String=row.get(1)?;if !values.contains(&parent){continue;}
        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6)",params![t,id,binding,row.get::<_,i64>(0)?,parent,row.get::<_,i64>(2)?])?;known.insert(parent);
    }
    super::dormant::capture_refs_preserving(tx,t,id,binding,kind,value,&known)
}

fn input_ref(key:&str,column:&str)->String {format!("$backfill:{key}:{column}")}

//! Server activation builder; all fallible work precedes commit.
use super::*;
impl Engine {
    pub(super) fn activate(
        store: &mut Store,
        schema: &Schema,
        programs: &BTreeMap<String, Program>,
        release: Option<&release::ReleaseActivation>,
        now: i64,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
        expected_release: Option<u64>,
        expected_backend: Option<&crate::store::opening::Backend>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, crate::store::opening::Metadata), RuntimeError> {
        schema.validate_column_owners()?;
        for program in programs.values() {
            crate::schema::ephemeral::admit_program(schema, program)?;
            admission::check_state_predicates(schema, program)?;
        }
        for program in programs.values() {
            for table in crate::ir::read_sites(program)
                .into_iter()
                .map(|s| s.table)
                .chain(crate::ir::write_tables(program).into_iter().map(|(t, _)| t))
            {
                schema.refuse_private(&table)?;
            }
        }
        for schedule in &schema.schedules {
            if !schedule.valid()
            {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
                    "schedule {} has an invalid period or offset",
                    schedule.name
                )));
            }
            let Some(program) = programs
                .get(&schedule.target)
                .filter(|p| matches!(p.kind, ProgramKind::Job | ProgramKind::Action))
            else {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
                    "schedule {} names missing job or action {}; restore or remove the schedule",
                    schedule.name, schedule.target
                )));
            };
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action {
                let seat = schedule
                    .service
                    .as_deref()
                    .filter(|seat| crate::identity::is_service(seat))
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        RuntimeError::Schema(
                            "a scheduled action requires its declared service seat".into(),
                        )
                    })?;
                actions::authorize(program, seat)?;
            } else if schedule.service.is_some() {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                    "a scheduled job cannot name a service seat".into(),
                ));
            }
            if program.args.iter().any(|(name, kind)| {
                !matches!(name.as_str(), "periodStart" | "periodEnd")
                    || *kind != crate::ir::ArgType::Time
            }) {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                    "scheduled work takes periodStart: time and periodEnd: time, or nothing".into(),
                ));
            }
        }
        let prior_items = std::cell::RefCell::new(Vec::new());
        let population=std::cell::Cell::new(None);
        let outcome=store.activate_at_with_launch(schema, Some(programs), false, now, allow_feed_adoption, |conn| {
            Store::check_selected_release(conn, expected_release)?;
            if let Some(backend) = expected_backend { backend.check(conn)?; }
            if let Some(options)=release {
                options.check(conn)?;
                *prior_items.borrow_mut()=crate::store::release_history::inventory(conn,None,usize::MAX)?;
                crate::store::dormant::enable(conn)?;
                conn.execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key,value) VALUES('release_native_digest',?1) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value",[options.native_digest.as_deref().unwrap_or("")])?;
                conn.execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key,value) VALUES('activation_restore',?1) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value",[if options.restore_dormant {"true"}else{"false"}])?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }, |tx, changed, report| {
            let now=now.max(tx.time_audience_clock()?);
            if let Some(options)=release {tx.set_meta("release",&options.release.to_string())?;}
            use rusqlite::OptionalExtension;
            let repair_live_sums = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'live_sum_candidates'", [], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.as_deref() != Some("1");
            let previous: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation = (SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation')", [], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
            let previous: Option<Schema> = previous.map(|text| serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::decode(e, RuntimeError::Store))).transpose()?;
            let needs_maintained_id_marker = (schema.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none())
                || previous.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| old.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none())))
                && tx.meta(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META)?.as_deref() != Some(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION);
            if !changed && !repair_live_sums && !needs_maintained_id_marker { return Ok(()); }
            let repair_maintained_ids = needs_maintained_id_marker
                && previous.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| old.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none()));
            if changed {
                let epoch = tx.next_seq()?;
                for table in partition::changed_group_audiences(previous.as_ref(), schema) {
                    tx.set_meta(&partition::audience_epoch_key(&table), &epoch.to_string())?;
                    tx.set_meta(&partition::audience_epoch_time_key(&table), &now.to_string())?;
                }
            }
            let population_changed=tx.meta("activation_population")?.as_deref()==Some("true");
            let rebuild: Vec<_> = schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| {
                (changed && (population_changed || previous.as_ref().is_none_or(|old| crate::successor::maintain_rebuild_needed(old, schema, m))))
                    || (repair_maintained_ids && m.row_key.is_none())
                    || (repair_live_sums && matches!(m.kind, crate::schema::MaintainKind::Sum { .. }) && schema.table(&m.source).is_some_and(|source| source.is_expiring()))
            }).collect();
            tx.set_meta("live_sum_candidates", "1")?;
            if needs_maintained_id_marker {
                // This write is inside the activation transaction: a failed
                // rebuild leaves both old rows and the old/missing marker. A
                // first aggregate has no legacy target identity to rewrite,
                // but records the current address function for later opens.
                tx.set_meta(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META, crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION)?;
            }
            let attribution = recorded::paths_changed(schema, previous.as_ref());
            let columns = column_activation::changed_parents(schema, if population_changed {None} else {previous.as_ref()});
            if rebuild.is_empty() && !attribution && columns.is_empty() && !repair_maintained_ids { return Ok(()); }
            let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
            let phase = std::time::Instant::now();
            if attribution {
                // This runner owns only activation rewrites; ordinary rebuilds
                // below retain their commit and partition accounting.
                let mut capture = Runner::new(tx, schema, Context::system().at(now), Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort), seq);
                capture.capture_activation(previous.as_ref(), Some(report))?;
                report.detail("capture recorded paths", phase);
                // A newly introduced aggregate has no legacy identity map to
                // preserve. Keep its event-free recorded-path rebuild out of
                // commit accounting, as activation did before the address
                // migration existed.
                if !repair_maintained_ids {
                    capture.rebuild_recorded_activation(previous.as_ref(), &rebuild.iter().copied().filter(|m| m.private.is_none()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), report)?;
                }
            }
            let mut runner = Runner::new(tx, schema, Context::system().at(now), Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort), seq);
            if repair_maintained_ids {
                let started = std::time::Instant::now();
                let rewritten = runner.rewrite_legacy_maintained_references(&rebuild.iter().copied().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>(), previous.as_ref(), Some(tx))?;
                if rewritten != 0 { report.changes.push(format!("rewrote {rewritten} legacy aggregate references")); }
                report.detail("rewrite legacy aggregate references", started);
            }
            // Legacy maps must be captured before any activation rebuild can
            // remove their target rows. Recorded values themselves are
            // stamped first so their historical paths may still traverse the
            // predecessor addresses.
            if attribution && repair_maintained_ids {
                runner.rebuild_recorded_activation(previous.as_ref(), &rebuild.iter().copied().filter(|m| m.private.is_none()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), report)?;
            }
            runner.activate_columns(previous.as_ref(), &rebuild, &columns, Some(report))?;
            for maintain in rebuild.into_iter().filter(|m| m.private.is_none()) {
                if !recorded::table_paths_changed(schema.table(&maintain.source), previous.as_ref().and_then(|s| s.table(&maintain.source))) {
                    runner.rebuild_maintain_reported(maintain, report)?;
                }
            }
            let events = runner.events.take();
            let tables: Vec<String> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
            let written = runner.rows_written;
            drop(runner);
            if written > 0 || !columns.is_empty() {
                tx.record_commit(seq, now, "rebuild", crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL, "rebuild", &tables, written)?;
                partition::append(tx, seq, &events)?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }, |tx| {
            if let Some(options)=release {
                crate::store::release_history::finalize_restorations(tx)?;
                let mut items=crate::store::release_history::inventory(tx.conn(),None,usize::MAX)?;
                let dormant_items=items.len();
                let mut restored_items=0usize;
                for item in &mut items {
                    let before=prior_items.borrow().iter().find(|i|i.key==item.key).cloned();
                    let count=before.as_ref().map_or(0,|i|i.rows);
                    item.before_rows=Some(count);item.after_rows=Some(item.rows);
                    item.before_sample_ids=Some(before.map_or_else(Vec::new,|i|i.sample_ids));
                    let restored=if item.kind=="rows" {
                        tx.meta(&format!("activation_narrowed_restored:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)
                    } else if item.kind=="column" {tx.meta(&format!("activation_column_restored:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)} else {0};
                    item.restored_rows=Some(restored);
                    if restored>0 {item.action="partially restored".into();restored_items+=1;}
                }
                let remaining:std::collections::BTreeSet<_>=items.iter().map(|i|i.key.clone()).collect();
                for mut item in prior_items.borrow().iter().filter(|i|!remaining.contains(&i.key)).cloned() {
                    let restored=if matches!(item.kind.as_str(),"rows"|"column") {
                        let prefix=if item.kind=="rows" {"activation_narrowed_restored"} else {"activation_column_restored"};
                        tx.meta(&format!("{prefix}:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)
                    } else if item.kind=="table" {
                        tx.meta(&format!("activation_table_restored:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)
                    } else {item.rows};
                    item.action=if restored>0 || item.definition_only {"restored"} else {"removed without restoration"}.into();
                    item.before_rows=Some(item.rows);item.after_rows=Some(0);item.restored_rows=Some(restored);item.before_sample_ids=Some(item.sample_ids.clone());
                    if restored>0 || item.definition_only {restored_items+=1;}
                    items.push(item);
                }
                population.set(Some((tx.meta("activation_narrowed_retained")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),tx.meta("activation_narrowed_restored")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0))));
                crate::store::release_history::event(tx,&options.operation,Some(options.release),options.expected_previous,serde_json::json!({"dormant_items":dormant_items,"restored_items":restored_items,"native_digest":options.native_digest,"narrowed_retained":tx.meta("activation_narrowed_retained")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),"narrowed_restored":tx.meta("activation_narrowed_restored")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)}),&items)?;
                tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key IN ('activation_restore','activation_population','activation_narrowed_retained','activation_narrowed_restored') OR key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored_ids:%'",[])?;
            }
            crate::store::opening::Metadata::read(tx)
        });
        if outcome.is_ok() {
            if let Some((retained,restored))=population.get().filter(|(a,b)|*a>0 || *b>0) {
                store.adoption_report.changes.insert(0,format!("{retained} newly dormant {}; {restored} narrowed {} restored", if retained == 1 { "row" } else { "rows" }, if restored == 1 { "row" } else { "rows" }));
            }
        }
        if outcome.is_ok() {
            if let Ok(items)=store.dormant_inventory(None,usize::MAX) {
                if release.is_some() { store.adoption_report.changes.insert(0, crate::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::dormant(&items)); }
            }
        }
        outcome
    }
}
//! A development reset: the adopted schema over an empty store, under a new
//! store identity, optionally reseeded, in one SQLite transaction.
// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.25 — what "fresh" keeps and what it empties; why the head keeps counting.
use super::*;

/// Tables a reset keeps whole: the store's own metadata, the catalog that
/// numbers tables and indexes, and the adopted generations.
const KEPT: [&str; 3] = ["_meta", "_catalog", "_generations"];

/// Identity tables a reset empties except for service seats: the dev
/// worker's session and `snapback4 token` seats outlive a reset, accounts,
/// guests and their sessions do not.
const SERVICE_SEATS: [(&str, &str); 5] = [
    ("_principals", "id"), ("_sessions", "principal"), ("_read_sessions", "principal"),
    ("_principal_generations", "principal"), ("_session_mint_bindings", "principal"),
];

/// What a development reset did. The caller unlinks `assets` after the
/// commit, outside any engine guard it holds.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct DevReset {
    pub previous_store_id: String,
    pub store_id: String,
    /// The store's head after the reset and any reseed.
    pub seq: u64,
    pub seeded: u64,
    pub assets: assets::AssetSweep,
}

impl Engine {
    /// Empty every application table and everything derived from them
    /// (views, maintained columns, indexes, counts, the partition log),
    /// every queue (effects, jobs, actions), uploaded assets and every
    /// non-service account and session; keep the adopted schema and
    /// generation. The store takes a new identity so a connected device
    /// re-acquires instead of keeping its partition; the head keeps counting
    /// (the reset is itself a commit). `rows` are then written as `/seed`
    /// writes them, in the same transaction. Development only: the release
    /// host never calls this.
    pub fn dev_reset_at(&mut self, rows: Vec<(String, Value)>, now: i64) -> Result<DevReset, RuntimeError> {
        // Like adoption: no ephemeral program runs across the identity change.
        drop(self.ephemeral.lock()?);
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#2-fixture-semantics — the new store identity must not inherit the retired store's future clock.
        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key = 'time_audience_clock'", [])?;
        let now = time_audience::pin_writer_time(&self.schema, &tx, now)?;
        let previous_store_id = tx.meta("store_id")?.unwrap_or_default();
        let head = tx.next_seq()?.saturating_sub(1);
        let assets: Vec<String> = tx.conn().prepare("SELECT id FROM _assets")?.query_map([], |row| row.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
        let tables: Vec<String> = tx.conn()
            .prepare("SELECT name FROM main.sqlite_schema WHERE type = 'table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite\\_%' ESCAPE '\\'")?
            .query_map([], |row| row.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.039#6.5 — every table empties together: no referential action runs, and no reference is left naming a removed row.
        for table in &tables {
            if KEPT.contains(&table.as_str()) { continue; }
            let quoted = format!("\"{}\"", table.replace('"', "\"\""));
            match SERVICE_SEATS.iter().find(|(name, _)| name == table) {
                Some((_, column)) => tx.conn().execute(&format!("DELETE FROM {quoted} WHERE substr({column}, 1, ?2) <> ?1"),
                    rusqlite::params![crate::identity::SERVICE_PREFIX, crate::identity::SERVICE_PREFIX.len() as i64])?,
                None => tx.conn().execute(&format!("DELETE FROM {quoted}"), [])?,
            };
        }
        // Prime at the reset clock in this transaction: no old period fires,
        // and the next crossed boundary fires normally. Replay's count restarts.
        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key LIKE 'schedule:%' OR key LIKE 'schedule-shape:%' OR key = 'dev:suppressed-effects'", [])?;
        for schedule in &self.schema.schedules {
            tx.set_meta(&format!("schedule:{}", schedule.name), &schedule.boundary(now).to_string())?;
            tx.set_meta(&format!("schedule-shape:{}", schedule.name), &crate::value::digest(schedule))?;
        }
        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _meta SET value = lower(hex(randomblob(16))) WHERE key = 'store_id'", [])?;
        let store_id = tx.meta("store_id")?.unwrap_or_default();
        // Every table changed: no change evidence may certify an interval
        // across the reset.
        tx.changes.borrow_mut().widen();
        let touched: Vec<String> = self.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, _)| name.clone()).collect();
        tx.record_commit(head + 1, now, "dev reset", crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL, "reset", &touched, 0)?;
        if crate::store::backfills::has_evidence(&tx)? {crate::store::backfills::prepare_inputs(&tx,&self.schema,None)?;}
        let seeded = seed_rows(&self.schema, &tx, rows, now)?;
        let seq = tx.next_seq()?.saturating_sub(1);
        tx.commit()?;
        // A new store identity starts a fresh bounded expiry walk. Clear only
        // after commit so a refused reset keeps the old store's continuation.
        self.expiry_after.clear();
        self.timeline_schedule = timeline::Schedule::default();
        self.ephemeral.rebind_committed(store_id.clone(), self.generation, &self.schema);
        Ok(DevReset { previous_store_id, store_id, seq, seeded, assets: assets::AssetSweep::named(assets, self.store.assets_dir()) })
    }
}
//! Bind historical arguments without requiring them in the current row type.
use super::*;
use crate::{ast, diag::{type_error, unknown}, types::{Ty, scalar_to_column}};
use snapback4_core::schema::backfill::Backfill;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &mut Schema, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    let mut names = BTreeSet::new(); let mut outputs = BTreeSet::new();
    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d| &d.backfills) {
        if !names.insert((d.table.clone(),d.name.clone())) { diagnostics.push(unknown(&d.site,"duplicate backfill name on this table","give each historical transform its own name")); continue; }
        let decl = ast::ProgramDecl { delivery: None, held:false, predicts:false, server:false, kind:ast::ProgramKind::Query,
            name: format!("backfill{}{}",d.table,d.name), args:d.args.clone(), prelude:vec![],
            body:vec![ast::Stmt::Return { value:d.value.clone(), site:d.site.clone() }], native:false, site:d.site.clone() };
        let Some(bound) = program::Binder::bind(catalog,schema,"schema",&decl,diagnostics) else {continue};
        let Ty::Object(fields) = &bound.result else { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,"backfill returns an object patch","{ column: historicalValue }")); continue; };
        let Some(table) = schema.table(&d.table) else {diagnostics.push(unknown(&d.site,"backfill table is not declared","declare the candidate table"));continue};
        for (c,ty) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(c) {
                if !ty.assignable_to(&Ty::from_column(kind)) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("backfill {}.{c} returns {}, expected {}",d.table,ty.name(),kind),"return a value fitting the candidate column")); }
            }
            if !outputs.insert((d.table.clone(),c.clone())) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("backfills overlap on {}.{c}",d.table),"combine simultaneous outputs in one backfill")); }
        }
        let fill = Backfill { table:d.table.clone(), name:d.name.clone(), inputs:d.args.iter().map(|a|(a.name.clone(),scalar_to_column(&a.type_))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>(), outputs:fields.keys().cloned().collect(), program:bound.program };
        if let Err(why) = fill.admit(schema) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,why,"use deterministic historical fields and a scalar patch").guide("deploy")); }
        schema.backfills.push(fill.normalized());
    }
}

fn collect_reads(body:&[crate::ir::Stmt],out:&mut BTreeSet<(String,String)>){
    use crate::ir::{Expr,Stmt};
    fn expr(e:&Expr,out:&mut BTreeSet<(String,String)>){match e{
        Expr::Scan(r)=>{out.insert((r.table.clone(),r.index.clone()));for k in &r.prefix{if let Some(e)=k.scalar(){expr(e,out);}}if let Some(range)=&r.range {for e in [&range.gt,&range.gte,&range.lt,&range.lte].into_iter().flatten(){expr(e,out);}}if let Some(e)=&r.filter{expr(e,out);}},
        Expr::Get(r)|Expr::Exists(r)=>{out.insert((r.table.clone(),r.index.clone()));for e in &r.key{expr(e,out);}},
        Expr::Field{base,..}|Expr::Unary{value:base,..}=>expr(base,out),Expr::Array(es)|Expr::Builtin{args:es,..}=>{for e in es{expr(e,out);}},
        Expr::Object(fs)=>{for e in fs.values(){expr(e,out);}},Expr::Shape{base,fields}=>{expr(base,out);for e in fields.values(){expr(e,out);}},
        Expr::Binary{left,right,..}=>{expr(left,out);expr(right,out);},Expr::Block{body,value}=>{collect_reads(body,out);expr(value,out);},_=>()
    }}
    for s in body{match s{Stmt::Let{value,..}|Stmt::Const{value,..}|Stmt::Assign{value,..}|Stmt::Return(value)=>expr(value,out),Stmt::Require{condition,..}=>expr(condition,out),Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body}=>{expr(condition,out);collect_reads(then_body,out);collect_reads(else_body,out);},_=>()}}
}

// Prune forgets historical values, retaining only the fact that exact reversal
// is no longer available. No tombstone can be queried by an app.
pub(super) fn prune(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    if present(tx)? {tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_evidence.t AND r.id=_backfill_evidence.id); DELETE FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_pruned.t AND r.id=_backfill_pruned.id); INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_pruned(t,id,definition,active,retained_outputs) SELECT t,id,definition,active,retained_outputs FROM _backfill_evidence; DELETE FROM _backfill_evidence; DELETE FROM _backfill_absence WHERE (definition!='' AND definition NOT LIKE '$native:%') OR NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_absence.t AND r.id=_backfill_absence.id);")?;}
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn inventory_sql(conn:&Connection)->Result<&'static str,RuntimeError>{
    let exists:bool=conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_backfill_evidence')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    Ok(if exists {" UNION ALL SELECT c.name||'.backfill#'||substr(e.definition,1,12),'backfill',c.name,e.definition FROM _backfill_evidence e JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=e.t GROUP BY e.t,e.definition "}else{""})
}

pub(crate) fn has_evidence(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{present(tx)}
pub(super) fn columns(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema)->Result<BTreeMap<String,BTreeSet<String>>,RuntimeError>{
    let mut out:BTreeMap<String,BTreeSet<String>>=BTreeMap::new();
    for f in &next.backfills {out.entry(f.table.clone()).or_default().extend(f.outputs.iter().cloned());}
    if present(tx)? {
        let mut statement=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition FROM _backfill_definitions WHERE digest IN (SELECT definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=1 OR retained_outputs!='[]' UNION SELECT definition FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE active=1 OR retained_outputs!='[]')")?;
        for text in statement.query_map([],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))? {
            let f:Backfill=decode(&text?)?;out.entry(f.table).or_default().extend(f.outputs);
        }
    }
    Ok(out)
}
fn validate_lookup(tx:&Tx<'_>,ti:i64,prefix:usize,table:&str,index:&str)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT k,id FROM i WHERE ti=?1 ORDER BY k")?;let mut rows=stmt.query([ti])?;
    let mut prior=None;let mut first=String::new();let mut duplicate=false;let mut bad=super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
    while let Some(row)=rows.next()? {
        let key:Vec<u8>=row.get(0)?;let id:String=row.get(1)?;let mut group=crate::key::decode(&key).ok_or_else(||refusal("invalid historical lookup key"))?;group.truncate(prefix);
        if group.contains(&Value::Null){continue;}
        if prior.as_ref()==Some(&group) {
            if !duplicate {bad.add(&first,"duplicate historical lookup key");duplicate=true;}
            bad.add(&id,"duplicate historical lookup key");
        }else{prior=Some(group);first=id;duplicate=false;}
    }
    if bad.count>0{return Err(refusal(format!("historical lookup {table}.{index} is not unique: {}; prepare a unique bridge before deriving values",bad.describe())))}
    Ok(())
}

// Each original adoption cohort saw one snapshot. Re-entry restores that same
// view across all its declarations; later cohorts read earlier cohorts' outputs.
fn restore_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=(SELECT o.data FROM temp._backfill_originals o WHERE o.t=r.t AND o.id=r.id) WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM temp._backfill_originals o WHERE o.t=r.t AND o.id=r.id)",[])?;Ok(())
}
// A reversed, originally absent cell must have the same meaning in explicit
// arguments, point lookups and lookup indexes. Null is a temporary source-view
// marker; the saved physical image is restored before committing any patch.
fn mask_unprovided(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT r.t,r.id,octet_length(r.data) FROM r JOIN temp._backfill_unprovided m ON m.t=r.t AND m.id=r.id ORDER BY r.t,r.id")?;
    let mut rows=q.query([])?;
    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;
        let mut meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);meter.heap((r.get::<_,i64>(2)?.max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
        let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;
        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
        if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {
            let mut columns=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT column_name FROM temp._backfill_unprovided WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?;
            for c in columns.query_map(params![t,id],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))? {fields.entry(c?).or_insert(Value::Null);}
        }
        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
    }Ok(())
}
// Recorded later cohorts originally saw earlier releases' completed patches.
// Replaying those older cohorts virtually preserves that ordering; fresh
// declarations still run first against the untouched predecessor snapshot.
fn replay_earlier_cohorts(tx:&Tx<'_>,epoch:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,octet_length(patch),patch FROM temp._backfill_pending WHERE CAST(json_extract(evidence,'$.epoch') AS INTEGER)<?1 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
    let mut rows=stmt.query([epoch])?;
    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;
        let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
        let Some(bytes)=bytes else{continue};
        let mut admission=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);admission.heap((bytes.max(0) as u64).saturating_add(r.get::<_,i64>(2)?.max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
        let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;
        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
        let patch:BTreeMap<String,Value>=decode(&r.get::<_,String>(3)?)?;
        if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {fields.extend(patch);}
        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
    }Ok(())
}

//! Operator preflight runs the real activation on a private disk-backed snapshot.
// REL3 owns dormant-visibility counts here; SURF clause narration lives in release_narration.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#92-exact-preview-and-operator-vocabulary — no live pointer, generation or audit changes.
use super::*;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
struct Snapshot(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Snapshot {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
    }
}

pub(super) fn preview(
    store_path: &Path,
    root: PathBuf,
    release: u64,
    options: &Options,
    held_owner: Option<&crate::store_owner::Owner>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let bound = super::releases::bound_at(root.clone())?;
    let project = Project { root };
    let native = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project,&bound.schema)?;
    let id = snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default())
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let snapshot = Snapshot(std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-release-preview-{id}")));
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&snapshot.0).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let path = snapshot.0.join("store.sqlite");
    // Probe the existing filesystem lock, independently of any HTTP address
    // hint. Keep a stopped store excluded through preview; never create a lock
    // sidecar or mutate its address record just to inspect ownership.
    let (live,_stopped_guard)=if held_owner.is_some() {(false,None)} else if store_path.exists() {
        let database=store_path.canonicalize().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        match snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(&database,&snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_path(&database),false,0) {
            Ok(owner)=>match owner.try_lock() {
                Ok(())=>(false,Some(owner)),
                Err(std::fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock)=>(true,None),
                Err(std::fs::TryLockError::Error(e))=>return Err(format!("preview owner probe failed: {e}")),
            },
            Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound=>return Err("preview cannot establish exclusive store ownership: missing owner sidecar; restart serve once, stop it, then retry".into()),
            Err(e)=>return Err(format!("preview owner probe failed: {e}")),
        }
    }else{(false,None)};
    // SQLite VACUUM INTO takes a coherent read snapshot even with a live WAL writer.
    // Busy/disk errors refuse this preview; never fall back to copying database bytes.
    if store_path.exists() {
    let source = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(store_path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    source
        .conn()
        .execute("VACUUM INTO ?1", [path.to_string_lossy().as_ref()])
        .map_err(|e| {
            format!("snapshot preview failed (retry after store/disk becomes available): {e}")
        })?;
    drop(source);
    }
    let mut store = snapback4_core::Store::open(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let held = store
        .release_status(0, None, None)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — preview uses the owning host's authoritative time, without minting or advancing anything.
    let activation_now=if live {
        let status=crate::release_control::request(store_path.parent().ok_or("preview store has no deployment directory")?,crate::release_control::Command::Status{after:0,items_after:None,event:None})?;
        if status["revision"]!=held["revision"] {return Err("preview raced release activation; retry against the current store".into());}
        status["clock"]["now"].as_i64().ok_or("serving owner does not expose its activation clock; restart it with this CLI before preview")?
    }else{snapback4_core::now_ms()};
    let mut activation =
        snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation::new(release, held["release"].as_u64());
    activation.expected_revision = held["revision"].as_u64();
    activation.native_digest = native.installed.map(|n| n.digest);
    activation.operation = "preview".into();
    activation.restore_dormant = !options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") || options.flags.contains_key("restore-dormant");
    let mut steps = Vec::new();
    let estimate = store
        .adoption_estimate(&bound.schema)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
        .map(|e| e.summary());
    let candidate_schema=bound.schema.clone();
    let candidate_programs:std::collections::BTreeMap<_,_>=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|(p.program.name.clone(),p.program.clone())).collect();
    let mut hazards=Vec::new();
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    let prior:Option<(String,String)>=store.conn().query_row("SELECT schema,programs FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')",[],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?))).ok();
    let prior_schema=prior.as_ref().map(|(schema,_)|serde_json::from_str::<snapback4_core::Schema>(schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).transpose()?;
    if let Some((schema,programs))=prior {
        let prior:snapback4_core::Schema=serde_json::from_str(&schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        steps=snapback4_core::successor::classify(&prior,&candidate_schema);
        let programs:std::collections::BTreeMap<String,snapback4_core::ir::Program>=serde_json::from_str(&programs).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        let target_label = format!("release {release}");
        let compatibility = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &prior, &candidate_schema, held["release"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0), &target_label, options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction"))?;
        for line in compatibility {
            if let Some(warning) = line.strip_prefix("WARNING ") { hazards.push(warning.to_owned()); }
            else { notes.push(line.trim_start_matches("note: ").to_owned()); }
        }
        let rollback=!options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction");
        let (behavior_warnings, behavior_notes) = super::release_narration::behavior(store_path, &store, &prior, &candidate_schema, &programs, &candidate_programs, &project, held["release"].as_u64(), &target_label, rollback)?;
        hazards.extend(behavior_warnings); notes.extend(behavior_notes);
        for name in prior.tables.keys().filter(|name|!candidate_schema.tables.contains_key(*name) && prior.maintain_for(name).is_none() && prior.tables[*name].ephemeral.is_none()) {
            let count:u64=store.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM r WHERE t=(SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1)",[name],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            if count>0 {hazards.push(format!("table {name}: {count} rows become dormant; {target_label} lacks this table's behavior; preserve it in a bridge or accept its absence"));}
        }
    }
    super::release_safeguards::capture(&store)?;
    let mut engine = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared_at(
        store,
        bound.schema,
        bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
        &activation,
        options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
        activation_now,
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut planned = engine
        .store_mut()
        .release_status(0, None, None)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    planned["items"] = serde_json::to_value(
        engine
            .store_mut()
            .dormant_inventory(None, usize::MAX)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let items: Vec<snapback4_core::store::DormantItem> = serde_json::from_value(planned["items"].clone()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut effects = super::release_safeguards::warnings(engine.store(),prior_schema.as_ref(),&candidate_schema,&candidate_programs,&items,release,!options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction"))?;
    effects.append(&mut hazards); hazards=effects;
    for fill in &engine.store().adoption_report.backfills { notes.push(format!("backfill: {fill}; confirm these historical values before selecting release {release}")); }
    steps.retain(|step|!step.message.contains("engine capture"));
    for step in &mut steps {
        if step.change==snapback4_core::successor::Change::Narrowing {
            step.change=snapback4_core::successor::Change::Additive;
            step.message=format!("{}; active rows satisfy the candidate after dormant projection",step.message.split(';').next().unwrap_or(&step.message));
        }
        if let Some(position)=step.path.strip_prefix("maintains[").and_then(|s|s.strip_suffix(']')).and_then(|s|s.parse::<usize>().ok()) {
            let label=candidate_schema.maintains[position].label();
            let visits=engine.store().adoption_report.views.iter().find(|view|view.name==label).map(|v|v.rows).unwrap_or(0);
            step.message=format!("maintain {label}: rebuilt from {visits} source rows");
            step.change=snapback4_core::successor::Change::Additive;
        }
        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.").unwrap_or(&step.path).replace(".columns.",".");
        let count=planned["items"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().find(|i|i["path"].as_str()==Some(path.as_str())).and_then(|i|i["rows"].as_u64()).unwrap_or(0);
        let unit = if step.path.contains(".columns.") { "cells" } else if step.path.contains(".indexes.") { "entries" } else { "rows" };
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — per-path prose uses the same disjoint counts as the inventory totals.
        let retained = if count == 0 && unit == "cells" {
            format!("no standalone {unit} become dormant (cells of any dormant rows are counted with those rows)")
        } else if count == 0 {
            format!("no {unit} become dormant")
        } else {
            format!("its {unit} become dormant ({count} {unit}; restored on forward)")
        };
        step.message=step.message.replace("its rows are lost", &retained).replace("its values are lost", &retained);
    }
    let checklist = super::release_bridge::checklist(held["release"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),release,&hazards);
    if let Some(path) = options.flags.get("bridge") {
        let current = held["release"].as_u64().ok_or("a bridge needs a current deployed release")?;
        super::release_bridge::write(Path::new(path),store_path.parent().ok_or("no deployment directory")?,current,release,&checklist)?;
        eprintln!("bridge starter written to {path}; edit its current source and follow ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md before activation");
    }
    if options.json {
        println!("{}",serde_json::to_string_pretty(&serde_json::json!({"preview":true,"checklist":checklist,"hazards":hazards,"notes":notes,"current":held,"plan":planned,"steps":steps,"estimate":estimate,"phases":engine.store().adoption_report.summary(),"backfills":engine.store().adoption_report.backfills})).unwrap());
    } else {
        println!("preview: release {release} would be current as generation {}; live release unchanged", engine.generation());
        super::release_summary::print(prior_schema.as_ref(), &candidate_schema, &candidate_programs, &hazards, &notes, &steps, &items);
        if !options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") { println!("  checklist: prove the named refusal cases and affected lists/counts as real accounts before release {release}; `--bridge <new-dir>` saves current source plus this app's ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md"); }
        println!("  storage: {}",snapback4_core::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::dormant(&items));
        if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {
            for hazard in hazards { println!("  detail: WARNING {hazard}"); }
            for note in notes { println!("  detail: note: {note}"); }
            for step in steps {println!("  {}: {}",step.path,step.message);}
            for item in &items { println!("  {}: {} {} dormant (restored on compatible forward); sample ids [{}]", item.key, item.rows, item.unit(), item.sample_ids.join(", ")); }
            println!("{}", engine.store().adoption_report.summary());
            if let Some(estimate) = estimate {
                println!("{estimate}");
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
//! Owner-mediated release publication in the running HTTP process.
use super::*;
use crate::release_control::Command;
#[cfg(unix)]
use crate::release_control::Listener;
use snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation;

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(super) fn start(
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    dir: std::path::PathBuf,
) -> Result<std::thread::JoinHandle<()>, String> {
    let store = shared
        .owner
        .as_ref()
        .ok_or("release control requires a persisted owner")?
        .path()
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let listener = Listener::bind(&dir, store)?;
    crate::lifecycle::managed_cleanup();
    crate::lifecycle::background("release-control", move || {
        listener.run(|command| apply(&shared, &dir, command))
    })
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — only assignments and committed rebind follow the database commit.
pub(super) fn apply(
    shared: &Shared,
    dir: &std::path::Path,
    command: Command,
) -> Result<Json, String> {
    match command {
        Command::Status {
            after,
            items_after,
            event,
        } => {
            let mut engine=lock(&shared.engine);
            let mut status=engine.store_mut().release_status(after,items_after.as_deref(),event).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            status["clock"]=json!({"now":shared.now(),"offsetMs":lock(&shared.clock).offset()});
            Ok(status)
        },
        Command::Prune {
            confirm,
            expected_revision,
        } => {
            let mut result = lock(&shared.engine)
                .store_mut()
                .prune_dormant(confirm, Some(expected_revision))
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if let Some(items) = result["items"].as_array_mut() {
                items.truncate(16);
            }
            result["items_after"] = result["items"]
                .as_array()
                .and_then(|items| items.last())
                .map(|i| i["key"].clone())
                .unwrap_or(Json::Null);
            Ok(result)
        }
        Command::Activate {
            release,
            expected_release,
            expected_revision,
            digest,
            rollback,
            restore_dormant,
            allow_feed_adoption,
        } => {
            if release == 0 {
                return Err("release id must be positive".into());
            }
            let project = Project {
                root: dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string()),
            };
            if !std::fs::symlink_metadata(&project.root)
                .is_ok_and(|m| m.is_dir() && !m.file_type().is_symlink())
            {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release {release} is not an immutable release directory"
                ));
            }
            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
                return Err("staged release content changed; redeploy its exact source".into());
            }
            if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release {release} has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release"
                ));
            }
            let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
                return Err(bound
                    .diagnostics
                    .iter()
                    .map(ToString::to_string)
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    .join("\n"));
            }
            let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
            crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
                return Err("release artifacts changed during preparation; retry after inspecting immutable source".into());
            }
            crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
            let mut pointer =
                crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, expected_release, rollback)?;
            let options = ReleaseActivation {
                release,
                expected_previous: expected_release,
                expected_revision: Some(expected_revision),
                restore_dormant,
                native_digest: prepared.installed.as_ref().map(|m| m.digest.clone()),
                operation: if rollback { "rollback" } else { "deploy" }.into(),
            };
            let _requests=shared.release_requests.write();
            let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
            lock(&shared.readers).begin_adoption(&bound);
            let generation = match engine.adopt_release_at(
                bound.schema.clone(),
                bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()),
                &options,
                allow_feed_adoption,
                shared.now(),
            ) {
                Ok(generation) => generation,
                Err(error) => {
                    lock(&shared.readers).finish_adoption();
                    return Err(error.to_string());
                }
            };
            #[cfg(test)]
            test_work(&shared.release_committed);
            engine.set_prepared_native(prepared.module);
            engine.set_presence(Some(shared.presence.clone()));
            shared.refresh_time_audience_clock(&engine, shared.now());
            lock(&shared.readers).replace(&engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
            {
                let mut active = lock(&shared.bound);
                let mut advertised = lock(&shared.generation);
                let mut native = lock(&shared.native);
                shared.latency.adopt(&bound);
                *active = bound;
                *advertised = generation;
                *native = prepared.installed;
                let mut root = lock(&shared.release_root);
                *root = Some(project.root);
                shared.worker_revision.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Release);
            }
            crate::lifecycle::generation(generation);
            shared.publish_engine(&engine);
            // Completion I/O can fail, but never abandons the new runtime.
            pointer.finish(generation)?;
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
                "snapback4 serve: release {release} activated without restart as generation {generation}"
            );
            Ok(
                json!({"release":release,"generation":generation,"revision":expected_revision+1,"hot":true,"adoption":engine.store().adoption_report.compact(),"details":engine.store().adoption_report.summary(),"backfills":engine.store().adoption_report.backfills}),
            )
        }
    }
}
