//! Development doors beside the operation routes: `POST /seed`, `POST /dev/reset`, and the
//! `--no-watch` hint that names a schema edited on disk after adoption.
use super::{Shared, lock, refusal_json, respond_timed};
use crate::project::{Project, SourceStamp};
use serde_json::{Value as Json, json};
use snapback4_core::error::Refusal;
use snapback4_core::wire;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Mutex;
use tiny_http::Request;

/// The source stamp each `--no-watch` dev host adopted, by project root.
static PINNED: Mutex<BTreeMap<PathBuf, SourceStamp>> = Mutex::new(BTreeMap::new());

pub(super) fn closed() -> Refusal {
    Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "development doors are closed; use serve staging with real users (guide staging), or explicitly opt into dev --unsafe-dev-doors for local fixtures").guide("staging")
}

pub(super) fn pin(project: &Path, stamp: SourceStamp) {
    PINNED.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).insert(project.to_owned(), stamp);
}

/// A `--no-watch` host serves the schema it started with. When a request
/// names an operation or argument that host does not know and the `.q` files
/// changed on disk since, say so: the edit is real, it was just never adopted.
pub(super) fn stale(shared: &Shared, refusal: Refusal) -> Refusal {
    let Some(adopted) = PINNED.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).get(&shared.project).copied() else { return refusal };
    if (Project { root: shared.project.clone() }).sources_stamp() == adopted { return refusal; }
    let message = format!("{}; this dev host runs with --no-watch and snapback/ changed on disk after it adopted its schema, so the edit is not served", refusal.message);
    Refusal { message, rewrite: Some("restart `snapback4 dev` (or drop --no-watch so saves re-adopt), then retry".into()), ..refusal }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#8 — persona names are not their principals; dev only.
pub(super) fn persona_argument(shared: &Shared, kinds: &BTreeMap<String,snapback4_core::ir::ArgType>, args: &Json) -> Option<Refusal> {
    if !shared.personas { return None; }
    let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
    crate::persona_principals::arguments(&bound.personas,kinds,args)
}
// Rows are written as the system principal; each names its id because seed is an upsert by id.
pub(super) fn seed(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    if !shared.dev_doors() || !request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|a| a.ip().is_loopback()) {
        respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "seeding is a development door")) }));
        return;
    }
    let refuse = |request, refusal: Refusal| respond_timed(request, shared, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal.guide("personas")) }));
    let rows: Vec<Json> = match serde_json::from_str(body) {
        Ok(Json::Array(rows)) => rows,
        _ => return refuse(request, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "seed takes a JSON array of {table, row}")),
    };
    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    if let Err(why) = shared.dev_authority(&engine) { drop(engine); return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})); }
    let now = shared.now();
    let typed = match typed_rows(engine.schema(), &rows, now) {
        Ok(typed) => typed,
        Err(refusal) => { drop(engine); return refuse(request, refusal); }
    };
    let outcome = engine.seed_at(typed, now);
    drop(engine);
    match outcome {
        Ok(count) => {
            shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine));
            respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({ "seeded": count }));
        }
        Err(error) => respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&seed_refusal(error)) })),
    }
}

/// `/seed` rows checked against the served schema and typed, or the first problem.
fn typed_rows(schema: &snapback4_core::Schema, rows: &[Json], now: i64) -> Result<Vec<(String, snapback4_core::Value)>, Refusal> {
    let mut typed = Vec::new();
    for (index, entry) in rows.iter().enumerate() {
        let (Some(table), Some(row)) = (entry.get("table").and_then(Json::as_str), entry.get("row")) else {
            return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "each seed entry is {table, row}"));
        };
        let Some(definition) = schema.table(table) else {
            return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("unknown table {table}")));
        };
        if row.is_object() && row.get("id").is_none_or(|id| !id.is_string()) {
            return Err(missing_id(index, table));
        }
        let mut row = row.clone();
        crate::verbs::test_fixture::relative_times(definition, &mut row, now)
            .map_err(|why| Refusal::new("E_FIXTURE_TIME", "input", format!("seed row {index} ({table}): {why}")))?;
        if let Some(why) = wire::bare_principal(definition, &row) {
            return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("seed row {index} ({table}): {why}")));
        }
        match wire::row_from_json(definition, &row) {
            Ok(value) => typed.push((table.to_owned(), value)),
            Err(why) => return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{table}: {why}"))),
        }
    }
    Ok(typed)
}

fn seed_refusal(error: snapback4_core::RuntimeError) -> Refusal {
    let mut refusal = error.refusal();
    // Reseeding with changed immutable values reads like a rule problem; say what happened.
    if refusal.code == "E_CONSTRAINT" && refusal.message.ends_with(" is immutable") {
        refusal.message.push_str("; /seed is an upsert by id, and a row with that id already exists with a different value in that column. Reseed it with its original value, give the row a new id, POST /dev/reset first, or restart dev with --memory for an empty store");
    }
    refusal
}

/// What `POST /dev/reset` was asked to do: the body is empty, a `/seed`
/// array, or `{"seed": [..], "clock": "reset"}` with either key optional.
#[derive(Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
struct ResetRequest { seed: Vec<Json>, clock: bool }

fn reset_request(body: &str) -> Result<ResetRequest, Refusal> {
    const SHAPE: &str = "reset takes no body, a /seed array [{table, row}, ..], or {\"seed\": [..], \"clock\": \"reset\"} with either key optional";
    if body.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(ResetRequest::default()); }
    match serde_json::from_str(body) {
        Ok(Json::Array(seed)) => Ok(ResetRequest { seed, clock: false }),
        Ok(Json::Object(fields)) => {
            let mut request = ResetRequest::default();
            for (key, value) in fields {
                match (key.as_str(), value) {
                    ("seed", Json::Array(seed)) => request.seed = seed,
                    ("clock", Json::String(clock)) if clock == "reset" => request.clock = true,
                    ("clock", Json::String(clock)) if clock == "keep" => request.clock = false,
                    ("clock", other) => return Err(crate::dev_clock::input(format!("reset's clock is \"reset\" (back to wall time) or \"keep\" (the default), not {other}"))),
                    (key, _) => return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{SHAPE}; got key {key:?}"))),
                }
            }
            Ok(request)
        }
        _ => Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", SHAPE)),
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.25 — one transaction: empty, new identity, reseed; then the host's caches follow.
pub(super) fn reset(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    if !shared.dev_doors() || !request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|a| a.ip().is_loopback()) {
        respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&reset_release()) }));
        return;
    }
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    let refuse = |request, refusal: Refusal| respond_timed(request, shared, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal.guide("testing")) }));
    let asked = match reset_request(body) {
        Ok(asked) => asked,
        Err(refusal) => return refuse(request, refusal),
    };
    // Serialize with clock advances, then take the engine before the clock:
    // the order clock advance and worker claims use.
    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
    let _advance = lock(&advance);
    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    if let Err(why) = shared.dev_authority(&engine) { drop(engine); return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})); }
    let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock).clone();
    if asked.clock && let Err(refusal) = clock.reset() { drop(engine); return refuse(request, refusal); }
    let now = clock.now();
    // Resolve against the prospective reset clock before changing any durable state.
    let typed = match typed_rows(engine.schema(), &asked.seed, now) {
        Ok(typed) => typed,
        Err(refusal) => { drop(engine); return refuse(request, refusal); }
    };
    let reset = match engine.dev_reset_at(typed, now) {
        Ok(reset) => reset,
        Err(error) => {
            drop(engine);
            return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&seed_refusal(error)) }));
        }
    };
    if asked.clock { *lock(&shared.clock) = clock; }
    *lock(&shared.dev_reset_store) = Some(reset.store_id.clone());
    // The reset committed a new identity and a new authority clock. Replace,
    // rather than max with, the retired store's cached high-water mark.
    shared.time_audience_clock.store(if engine.schema().tables.values().any(|t| t.not_before.is_some()) { now } else { i64::MIN }, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Release);
    // Detached readers reopen; the following registry, change answers and
    // `/dev/status` forget the old identity. Wake then names the new store,
    // so every held poll returns and each client sees the replacement.
    let sites = lock(&shared.bound).refusal_sites.clone();
    lock(&shared.readers).replace(&engine, &sites);
    lock(&shared.adoption).store_id = reset.store_id.clone();
    if let Ok(reader) = snapback4_core::engine::following_history::Reader::new() { *lock(&shared.following) = reader; }
    *lock(&shared.change_answers) = Default::default();
    shared.durable_committed(&engine);
    let generation = engine.generation();
    drop(engine);
    let assets = reset.assets.len();
    if let Err(error) = reset.assets.remove_files() { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: reset left asset bytes behind: {error}"); }
    let now = shared.now();
    snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: store reset (store {} → {}, seeded {}) in {} ms", reset.previous_store_id, reset.store_id, reset.seeded, started.elapsed().as_millis());
    respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({
        "reset": true, "seeded": reset.seeded, "generation": generation, "storeId": reset.store_id,
        "previousStoreId": reset.previous_store_id, "head": reset.seq, "assetsRemoved": assets,
        "now": now, "clockOffsetMs": lock(&shared.clock).offset(), "skewMs": now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "ms": started.elapsed().as_millis() as u64,
    }));
}

pub(super) fn reset_release() -> Refusal {
    Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "resetting the store is a development door; snapback4 serve never empties a store")
        .guide("testing")
}

fn missing_id(index: usize, table: &str) -> Refusal {
    Refusal::new("E_FIELD", "input", format!(
        "missing field id: seed row {index} ({table}) has no string id. /seed is an upsert by id, so every row names its own; \
         rerunning the same seed then updates those rows instead of duplicating them"))
        .rewrite("give each row a 26-character id in Crockford base32 (digits and capital letters except I, L, O, U), \
                  e.g. \"row\": {\"id\": \"0000000000000000000000SEED\", ..}; in JavaScript `idFrom(\"seed:alice\")` from \
                  snapback4/client derives the same valid id from a label every time, and a reference column takes that id")
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    #[test]
    fn the_missing_id_example_is_a_valid_id() {
        let refusal = super::missing_id(0, "notes");
        let rewrite = refusal.rewrite.unwrap();
        let example = rewrite.split("\"id\": \"").nth(1).unwrap().split('"').next().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(example.len(), 26, "{example}");
        assert!(snapback4_core::ids::minted_at(example).is_some(), "{example} is Crockford base32");
        assert!(refusal.message.starts_with("missing field id"), "{}", refusal.message);
    }
}
//! Host startup; request scheduling remains in serve.rs.
use super::*;
pub fn serve(
    project: Project,
    port: u16,
    mut mode: Mode,
    bind: &str,
    inherited: Option<std::net::TcpListener>,
    capacity: crate::host_limits::Capacity,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    crate::host_limits::initialize();
    let capacity = capacity.fit()?;
    crate::host_limits::install(capacity);
    let mut source_stamp = project.sources_stamp();
    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        let verbose = !matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { verbose: false, .. });
        let (reports, hidden) = crate::verbs::problem_reports(&bound, true, verbose);
        let shown = reports.len();
        for report in reports {
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{report}");
        }
        if hidden > 0 {
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
                "snapback4 dev: {hidden} redundant cascade diagnostic(s) hidden; use --verbose to show them"
            );
        }
        return Err(format!(
            "{shown} problem{} in snapback/",
            if shown == 1 { "" } else { "s" }
        ));
    }
    let dev = matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { .. });
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — replay suppresses new effects and holds schedule admission; ordinary holds keep their separate meanings.
    let replay = matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { replay: true, .. });
    let hold_effects = matches!(
        mode,
        Mode::Dev {
            hold_effects: true,
            ..
        }
    );
    let hold_schedules = replay
        || matches!(
            mode,
            Mode::Dev {
                hold_schedules: true,
                ..
            }
        );
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — only immutable store classification admits a release test clock.
    let staging = if let Mode::Release { owner, .. } = &mode {
        owner.open_store(Duration::from_secs(5)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.meta("deployment_environment").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() == Some("staging")
    } else { false };
    let clock = crate::dev_clock::Clock::new(
        dev || staging,
        match mode {
            Mode::Dev { clock_offset, .. } | Mode::Release { clock_offset, .. } => clock_offset,
        },
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let generated = if dev {
        Some(project.write_generated(&bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?)
    } else {
        None
    };
    if dev {
        project.check_server_at(&mut source_stamp)?;
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — only the owning host may replace replay/schedule policy; deployment shares the same lock.
    // Release startup already owns this lock before selecting its source.
    let mut host_lock = if matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { fresh: false, .. }) {
        Some(crate::store_owner::host(&project.store_path())?)
    } else {
        None
    };
    // A busy port is a failed start, not permission to change the stopped host's policy.
    let server =
        crate::witness_socket::server(bind, port, inherited, capacity.connections(), || {
            crate::listen_refusal::in_use(bind, port, if dev { "dev" } else { "serve" })
        })?;
    let address = server
        .server_addr()
        .to_ip()
        .ok_or("HTTP listener has no IP address")?;
    if let Some(file) = &mut host_lock {
        crate::store_owner::announce(file, address)?;
    }
    if let Mode::Release { owner, .. } = &mut mode {
        // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — publish through the acquired handle, including a release database symlink.
        crate::store_owner::announce(
            Arc::get_mut(owner).ok_or("release owner already shared")?,
            address,
        )?;
    }
    let host_owner = match &mode {
        Mode::Release { owner, .. } => Some(owner.clone()),
        _ => host_lock.take().map(Arc::new),
    };
    if let Some(owner) = &host_owner { owner.report_reclaim(matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { verbose: true, .. })); }
    // `--fresh` has private, disposable storage and the same WAL readers as dev.
    let fresh = if matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { fresh: true, .. }) {
        Some(query_readers::FreshDirectory::new()?)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let mut engine = match &mode {
        Mode::Dev { fresh: true, .. } => Engine::new_at(
            fresh.as_ref().unwrap().store().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
            bound.schema.clone(),
            bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()),
            clock.now(),
        ),
        Mode::Dev { .. } => adoption::open(host_owner.as_deref().unwrap(), &bound, &clock),
        Mode::Release { expected, .. } => host_owner
            .as_ref()
            .unwrap()
            .open_store(Duration::from_secs(5))
            .and_then(|store| project.open_release_engine(store, &bound, *expected, clock.now())),
    }
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    adoption::report(&engine);
    if dev {
        match crate::dev_clock_store::ahead(&engine, clock.now()) {
            Ok(Some(note)) => snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{note}"),
            Ok(None) => {}
            Err(error) => {
                snapback4_core::diagnostic!("clock     store-ahead inspection unavailable: {error}")
            }
        }
        for line in crate::dev_clock_store::held_schedules(&engine, clock.now()) {
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{line}");
        }
    }
    crate::dev_worker::clear_child_session(engine.store()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    // A native job's module (LLP 3000.005): built by `check`, in `dev` also
    // here so a fresh clone serves without a separate step.
    if dev {
        crate::native::build(&project, &bound).map_err(|p| p.to_string())?;
    }
    if dev {
        crate::dev_replay::effects(&engine, replay).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        crate::dev_replay::schedules(&mut engine, hold_schedules, clock.now())
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    if !hold_schedules {
        engine
            .fire_schedules(clock.now())
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    let installed = crate::native::install(&mut engine, &project).map_err(|p| p.to_string())?;
    let authoritative_now = engine
        .advance_time_audiences_at(clock.now())
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let time_audience_clock = if engine.schema().tables.values().any(|table| table.not_before.is_some()) {
        authoritative_now
    } else {
        i64::MIN
    };
    let clock = Arc::new(Mutex::new(clock));
    let presence = Arc::new(Live::new(clock.clone()));
    engine.set_presence(Some(presence.clone()));
    let seq = engine.seq().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let generation: u64 = engine.generation();
    let adoption = Mutex::new(adoption::Status::new(
        engine
            .store()
            .meta("store_id")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
            .unwrap_or_default(),
    ));
    // Keep registration custody in this serving frame, independently of worker Arcs.
    let registration = if dev { Some(crate::dev_hosts::register(&project.root, address,
        engine.store().meta("store_id").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.unwrap_or_default(),
        matches!(mode, Mode::Dev {fresh:true,..}))?) } else {None};
    let readers = Mutex::new(QueryReaders::new(&engine, &bound.refusal_sites));
    let shared = Arc::new(Shared {
        dev_reset_store: Mutex::new(None),
        dev_identity: registration.as_ref().map(|r| r.identity.clone()),
        release_requests: Arc::new(crate::release_requests::Gate::default()),
        #[cfg(test)]
        release_committed: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        worker_prepare: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        worker_minted: Mutex::new(None),
        following: Mutex::new(
            snapback4_core::engine::following_history::Reader::new().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
        ),
        project: project.root.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
        release_root: Mutex::new((!dev).then(|| project.root.clone())),
        temporary: matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { fresh: true, .. }),
        refusals: if dev {
            dev_refusals::Recent::start(
                &project.root,
                &engine
                    .store()
                    .meta("store_id")
                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
                    .unwrap_or_default(),
                address,
                matches!(
                    mode,
                    Mode::Dev {
                        trace_empty: true,
                        ..
                    }
                ),
                matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { verbose: true, .. }),
            )?
        } else {
            Default::default()
        },
        latency: Arc::new(latency::Registry::new(&bound)),
        ephemeral: ephemeral::hub(&engine),
        ephemeral_rate: ephemeral::Rate::default(),
        wake: Mutex::new(ephemeral::Wake::new(&engine)),
        polls: Condvar::new(),
        adoption,
        clock,
        time_audience_clock: AtomicI64::new(time_audience_clock),
        maintenance: FairMutex::gate(maintenance::State::default()),
        engine: FairMutex::new(engine),
        readers,
        read_cuts: Mutex::new(read_cost::ReadCuts::default()),
        bound: Mutex::new(bound),
        generation: Mutex::new(generation),
        worker_revision: AtomicUsize::new(0),
        committed: Condvar::new(),
        seq: Mutex::new(seq),
        personas: dev,
        unsafe_dev_doors: matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { unsafe_dev_doors: true, .. }),
        hold_effects,
        hold_schedules,
        entropy: Mutex::new(snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default()),
        presence,
        long_waits: long_waits::LongWaits::new(&capacity),
        change_answers: Mutex::default(),
        asset_transfers: AssetTransfers::default(),
        native: Mutex::new(installed),
        auth: crate::auth::Admission::default(),
        uploads: AtomicUsize::new(0),
        route_verification: routes::Verification::default(),
        owner: host_owner.clone(),
        #[cfg(test)]
        auth_work: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        request_work: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        upload_work: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        effect_wait: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        poll_wait: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        poll_resumed: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        poll_notified: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        query_wait: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        reader_open: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        changes_refresh: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        mutation_wait: Mutex::new(None),
    });
    shared.install_ephemeral_wake();
    if dev {
        crate::lifecycle::managed_cleanup();
    }
    let server = Arc::new(server);
    let address = server.server_addr();
    let worker_url = format!("http://{address}")
        .replace("0.0.0.0", "127.0.0.1")
        .replace("[::]", "[::1]");
    let worker = match &mode {
        Mode::Dev { .. } => {
            crate::dev_worker::DevWorker::start(&project, shared.clone(), worker_url.clone())?
        }
        Mode::Release {
            worker: Some(source),
            ..
        } => Some(crate::dev_worker::DevWorker::start_source(
            &project,
            shared.clone(),
            worker_url.clone(),
            source.clone(),
            true,
        )?),
        Mode::Release { .. } => match crate::dev_worker::source(&project) {
            Some(source) => Some(crate::dev_worker::DevWorker::start_source(&project, shared.clone(), worker_url.clone(), source, false)?),
            None => None,
        },
    };
    if worker.is_none() && crate::dev_worker::declared(&project)?.is_empty() {
        let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
        if !bound.schema.action_specs.is_empty() || !bound.schema.routes.is_empty() {
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("WARNING: declared actions/routes have no supervised handler entry; requests remain queued without an external handler. Add snapback/server.ts and deploy, or pass serve --worker <path>; doctor reports handler status.");
        }
    }
    let active_hash = lock(&shared.bound).schema.hash();
    let working_hash = std::env::current_dir().ok().and_then(|cwd| Project::locate(&cwd))
        .and_then(|working| working.compile().ok()).filter(|bound| bound.diagnostics.is_empty())
        .map(|bound| bound.schema.hash()).unwrap_or_else(|| "unavailable".into());
    let mut background = Vec::new();
    #[cfg(unix)]
    if !dev {
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — control belongs to immutable release source, even when its store is a symlink elsewhere.
        let dir = shared.project.parent().and_then(std::path::Path::parent)
            .ok_or("deployment parent")?.to_owned();
        background.push(("release-control", release::start(shared.clone(), dir)?));
    }
    crate::lifecycle::generation(generation);
    // The URL is stdout's first line; lifecycle and redacted worker logs use stderr.
    snapback4_core::diagnostic_stdout!("http://{address}");
    // @ref LLP 3000.011.004.000#6 — one tagged readiness line a script can match in merged stdout+stderr.
    let wrapper = crate::supervisor::pid()
        .map(|pid| format!(" wrapperPid={pid}"))
        .unwrap_or_default();
    snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
        "SNAPBACK4_READY verb={} generation={generation} url=http://{address} pid={}{wrapper}",
        if dev { "dev" } else { "serve" },
        std::process::id()
    );
    let wrapper = crate::supervisor::pid()
        .map(|pid| format!(", wrapper pid {pid}"))
        .unwrap_or_default();
    match &generated {
        // The PID lets a script that spawned `dev` (perhaps through npx) prove it owns the server it polls.
        Some(generated) => snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
            "snapback4 dev: http://{address}  pid {}{wrapper}  (schema: snapback/, types: {})",
            std::process::id(),
            generated.display()
        ),
        None => snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
            "snapback4 serve: http://{address}  pid {}{wrapper}  (release {}, generation {generation}, active schema {active_hash}, working schema {working_hash}, tokens only)",
            std::process::id(),
            project.root.display()
        ),
    }
    if dev {
        match mode {
            Mode::Dev { fresh: true, .. } => {
                snapback4_core::diagnostic!("store: temporary — discarded on exit")
            }
            _ => snapback4_core::diagnostic!("store: {}", project.store_path().display()),
        }
    }
    if dev {
        if shared.dev_doors() {
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("WARNING snapback4 dev: UNSAFE development doors enabled — /seed, /dev/* and declared personas admit unauthenticated loopback callers. Never use for real users or probe:serve; guide deploy staging.");
        } else {
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: password-only development doors CLOSED. Use deploy --staging + serve for real users; --unsafe-dev-doors explicitly enables local fixture tools.");
        }
    }
    if replay {
        snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
            "snapback4 dev: replay on — new effects suppressed; schedules held"
        );
    }
    if let Mode::Dev { watch: false, .. } = mode {
        dev_doors::pin(&shared.project, source_stamp);
        snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
            "snapback4 dev: --no-watch — schema edits are not re-adopted; restart dev (or drop --no-watch) to serve a changed snapback/"
        );
    }
    if let Mode::Dev {
        watch: true,
        verbose,
        ..
    } = mode
    {
        let shared = shared.clone();
        let project = Project {
            root: project.root.clone(),
        };
        background.push((
            "watcher",
            crate::lifecycle::background("watcher", move || {
                watch_sources(project, shared, source_stamp, verbose)
            })?,
        ));
    }
    {
        let shared = shared.clone();
        background.push((
            "maintenance",
            crate::lifecycle::background("maintenance", move || maintain(shared))?,
        ));
    }
    {
        let shared = shared.clone();
        background.push((
            "bulk-maintenance",
            crate::lifecycle::background("bulk-maintenance", move || maintenance::bulk(shared))?,
        ));
    }
    if dev {
        snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
            "snapback4 dev: loopback identity attempts have no per-peer or per-email minute limits; Argon2 slots: {} on this host; connection/worker caps still apply",
            shared.auth.kdf_slots(true)
        );
    }
    let worker =
        crate::dev_worker::Supervisor::start(project, shared.clone(), worker_url, dev, worker)?;
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — dev and serve drain startup banners before accepting work, bounded for stalled output.
    let _ = snapback4_core::diagnostics::drain(Duration::from_secs(3));
    serve_requests(
        server,
        shared,
        worker,
        background,
        crate::lifecycle::stopping,
        capacity,
    )
}

// The production accept loop is also exercised with a real supervised child
// and a held SQLite adoption transaction in the server regression.
pub(super) fn serve_requests(
    server: Arc<Server>, shared: Arc<Shared>, worker: crate::dev_worker::Supervisor,
    background: Vec<(&str, std::thread::JoinHandle<()>)>, stopping: impl Fn() -> bool,
    capacity: crate::host_limits::Capacity,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    // @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — bound HTTP admission while reserving short requests
    crate::thread_priority::dispatcher();
    let admission = Arc::new(RequestAdmission::new(&capacity));
    let mut reported = tiny_http::AdmissionRefusals::default();
    let mut reported_workers = ([0; CLASSES], [0; CLASSES]);
    let mut report_at = Instant::now();
    loop {
        if report_at.elapsed() >= Duration::from_secs(1) {
            let refusals = server.admission_refusals();
            if refusals != reported && snapback4_core::diagnostics::emit(format_args!(
                "E_RATE_LIMIT at http: all {} HTTP connections occupied at admission; cumulative refused={} http_written={} http_failed={} tls_closed={}; since_summary={}; wait for pending writes to settle or reduce concurrent clients; wire delivery is best-effort",
                capacity.connections(), refusals.total(), refusals.http_written, refusals.http_failed, refusals.tls_closed, refusals.total().saturating_sub(reported.total())), false) {
                reported = refusals;
            }
            let workers = (admission.refused.each_ref().map(|n| n.load(Ordering::Relaxed)), admission.undelivered.each_ref().map(|n| n.load(Ordering::Relaxed)));
            if workers != reported_workers && snapback4_core::diagnostics::emit(format_args!(
                "E_RATE_LIMIT at workers: cumulative refused={:?} undelivered={:?}; pools=[short,mutation,sync,wait,route,registry,verifier,claim]; full wait/claim pools answer E_STREAMS; reduce concurrent clients; wire delivery is best-effort", workers.0, workers.1), false) {
                reported_workers = workers;
            }
            report_at = Instant::now();
        }
        if stopping() {
            let address = server.server_addr();
            drop(server);
            // tiny_http's accept thread owns the descriptor until it observes
            // the close trigger. Confirm closure before reporting it.
            if let Some(mut socket) = address.clone().to_ip() {
                if socket.ip().is_unspecified() {
                    socket.set_ip(if socket.is_ipv4() { std::net::IpAddr::V4(std::net::Ipv4Addr::LOCALHOST) } else { std::net::IpAddr::V6(std::net::Ipv6Addr::LOCALHOST) });
                }
                let until = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(3);
                loop {
                    match std::net::TcpStream::connect_timeout(&socket, Duration::from_millis(50)) {
                        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::ConnectionRefused => break,
                        _ if Instant::now() >= until => return Err("HTTP socket closure could not be confirmed".into()),
                        _ => std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)),
                    }
                }
            }
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("SNAPBACK4_SOCKET_CLOSED verb={} url=http://{address} socket closed", if shared.personas { "dev" } else { "serve" });
            let _ = snapback4_core::diagnostics::drain(Duration::from_secs(3));
            shared.refusals.shutdown();
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#51-path-independent-control-address — retain store ownership until control drains admitted work and unlinks its socket.
            for (name, thread) in background {
                if name == "release-control" {
                    thread.join().map_err(|_| "release-control thread panicked during shutdown")?;
                }
            }
            if let Some(owner) = &shared.owner { owner.clear_address(); }
            return Ok(());
        }
        for (name, thread) in &background {
            if thread.is_finished() { return Err(format!("{name} thread stopped")); }
        }
        if worker.is_finished() { return Err("worker thread stopped".into()); }
        if let Some(request) = server.recv_timeout(Duration::from_millis(100)).map_err(crate::host_limits::listener_error)? {
            dispatch(request, shared.clone(), admission.clone(), server.server_addr().to_ip().is_some_and(|a| a.ip().is_loopback()));
        }
    }
}
