#[test]
fn removal_of_last_opening_boundary_keeps_the_durable_retirement_floor() {
 let old="table items:\n  opens: time\n  until: time\n  n: int\n  not before .opens\n  expire at .until\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  not before .opens\n","").replace("  expire at .until\n","")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 for requested in [1,100] {
  let(s,p)=compile(old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
  let r=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["items"],&json!({"id":"a","opens":0,"until":50,"n":3})).unwrap();e.seed_at([("items".into(),r)],0).unwrap();
  {let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();tx.pin_time_audience_clock(100).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}
  let(s,p)=compile(&next);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,requested).unwrap();
  assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0,"source deadline before persisted floor, requested={requested}");
 }
}

#[test]
fn a_new_named_declaration_transforms_existing_historical_inputs() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let twice=old.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let triple=twice.replace("twice(n:","triple(n:").replace("n * 2","n * 3");
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&triple,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(9));
}
#[test]
fn dropped_required_and_optional_inputs_survive_active_table_reentry() {
 for kind in ["int","int ?"] {
  let old=format!("table items:\n  n: {kind}\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");
  let next=old.replace(&format!("  n: {kind}"),"  doubled: int")+&format!("backfill items twice(n: {kind}):\n  {{doubled:(n ?? 0) * 2}}\n");
  let mut e=engine(&old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(6));
 }
}
#[test]
fn reentry_restores_shared_snapshots_for_arguments_and_point_reads() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let twice=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 for later in [false,true] {
  let copied=twice.replace("  n: int","  copied: int\n  lookedUp: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items copy(n: int, id: items):\n  {copied:n, lookedUp:(items[id] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));
  if later {adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();}
  adopt(&mut e,&copied,3).unwrap();let expected=if later {6}else{3};
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","copied"),Value::Int(expected));
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&copied,3).unwrap();
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(6));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","copied"),Value::Int(expected));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","lookedUp"),Value::Int(expected));
 }
}

#[test]
fn a_retained_transform_output_cannot_prove_native_origin() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let twice=old.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let broken=twice.replace("twice(n: int)","other(missing: int)").replace("doubled:n * 2","doubled:missing");
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&broken,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical input items.missing"),"{error}");
}

#[test]
fn partial_table_reentry_restores_carried_declarations_in_the_same_cohort() {
 let parents="table parents:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let old=parents.to_owned()+"table children:\n  parent: parents\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let parent_fill="backfill parents twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 let next=old.replace("  parent: parents","  copied: int\n  parent: parents")+parent_fill+"backfill children copy(parent: parents):\n  {copied:(parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
 let mut e=engine(&old);seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":3}));seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 adopt(&mut e,&(parents.to_owned()+parent_fill),3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"parents","p","n"),Value::Int(6));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"children","c","copied"),Value::Int(3));
}

#[test]
fn leaving_the_last_backfill_cannot_remove_an_ended_source_lifetime() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  until: time\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  until: time","  until: time\n  expire at .until")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 for absent in [false,true] {
  let(s,p)=compile(old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
  let r=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["items"],&json!({"id":"a","n":3,"until":50})).unwrap();e.seed_at([("items".into(),r)],0).unwrap();
  let(s,p)=compile(&next);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,1).unwrap();
  if absent {let(s,p)=compile("query empty():\n  return true\n");e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(3,Some(2)),false,2).unwrap();}
  let previous=if absent {3}else{2};let(s,p)=compile(old);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,Some(previous)),false,100).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0);
 }
}
#[test]
fn reentry_keeps_absence_distinct_from_a_later_default() {
 let old="table items:\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  public","  n: int default 100\n  public")+"backfill items one(n: int ?):\n  {n:(n ?? 0) + 1}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a"}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(1));
 adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(1));
 adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(1));
}
#[test]
fn saved_reference_inputs_keep_their_own_lifetime_after_live_retargeting() {
 for kind in ["parents","id"] {
 let parents="table parents:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let mutation="mutation retarget():\n  update children['c'] {parent:'q'}\n";
 let old=parents.to_owned()+"table children:\n  parent: parents on delete keep\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\n  online only\n"+mutation;
 let fill=format!("backfill children copy(parent: {kind}):\n  {{copied:(parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}}\n");
 let next=old.replace("  parent: parents","  copied: int\n  parent: parents")+&fill;
 let dropped=next.replace("  parent: parents on delete keep\n","").replace(mutation,"");
 let mut e=engine(&old);for(id,n)in[("p",3),("q",9)]{seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":id,"n":n}));}seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();write(&mut e,"retarget");adopt(&mut e,&dropped,3).unwrap();
 let table=e.schema().tables["parents"].clone();{let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let r=tx.get("parents","p").unwrap().unwrap();tx.delete("parents",&table,&r).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":77}));
 adopt(&mut e,parents,4).unwrap();let error=adopt(&mut e,&dropped,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("parent lifetime"),"{error}");
}
}

 adopt(&mut e,&next.replace("int 0..5","int 0..10"),3).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("children").unwrap(),0,"a retained output reference cannot recapture a reused parent");

#[test]
fn a_transformed_restored_row_keeps_its_new_cells_when_it_becomes_dormant_again() {
 let old="table parents:\n  n: int 0..10\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable children:\n  label: enum('a','b')\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let narrow=old.replace("enum('a','b')","enum('a')");
 let next=old.replace("  n: int 0..10","  n: int 0..5").replace("  label:","  link: parents\n  label:")+"backfill children migration(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2, link:(parents['p'] ! else NOT_FOUND).id}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":9}));seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","label":"b","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&narrow,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("children").unwrap(),0);
 adopt(&mut e,&next.replace("int 0..5","int 0..10"),4).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"children","c","n"),Value::Int(6));
}

#[test]
fn native_images_survive_optional_missing_inputs_and_constant_backfills() {
 for optional in [false,true] {
  let old="table items:\n  n: int ?\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
  let next=old.replace("  n: int ?","  derived: int")+if optional {"backfill items derive(n: int ?):\n  {derived:(n ?? 0) * 2}\n"}else{"backfill items derive():\n  {derived:0}\n"};
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"native","derived":9}));
  adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","native","derived"),Value::Int(9));
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","native","derived"),Value::Int(9));
 }
}
#[test]
fn invalid_present_inputs_never_qualify_as_native_missing_inputs() {
 let v2="table items:\n  n: bool\n  derived: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let old=v2.replace("  derived: int\n","");
 let next=v2.to_owned()+"backfill items derive(n: int, missing: int ?):\n  {derived:n + (missing ?? 0)}\n";
 let mut e=engine(v2);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":true,"derived":9}));adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical input items.n"),"{error}");
}
#[test]
fn original_absence_survives_later_defaults_until_a_meaningful_input_edit() {
 let old="table items:\n  label: text <=10\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\n  online only\n";
 let fill="backfill items derive(n: int ?):\n  {derived:n ?? 0}\n";
 let next=old.replace("  label:","  derived: int\n  label:")+fill;
 let later=next.replace("  label:","  n: int default 100\n  label:")+"mutation edit(n: int):\n  update items['a'] {n}\nmutation label():\n  update items['a'] {label:'changed'}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","label":"old"}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&later,3).unwrap();write(&mut e,"label");
 adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&later,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","derived"),Value::Int(0));
 for n in [200,100] {let r=e.mutate("edit",None,Context::new("alice",std::collections::BTreeMap::from([("n".into(),Value::Int(n))]))).unwrap();assert!(matches!(r.state,WriteState::Sent{..}),"{r:?}");}
 adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&later,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","derived"),Value::Int(100));
}

#[test]
fn a_new_declaration_name_does_not_inherit_another_native_definition() {
 let old="table items:\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  public","  derived: int\n  public")+"backfill items zero():\n  {derived:0}\n";
 let newer=next.replace("items zero()","items one()").replace("derived:0","derived:1");
 let mut e=engine(old);adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"native","derived":9}));adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&newer,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","native","derived"),Value::Int(1));
}

#[test]
fn native_authored_outputs_survive_matching_inputs_and_refuse_changed_inputs() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nmutation edit():\n  update items['native'] {n:4}\n";
 let next=old.replace("  n: int","  derived: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {derived:n * 2}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"native","n":3,"derived":9}));adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","native","derived"),Value::Int(9));
 adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();write(&mut e,"edit");let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("native backfill inputs changed"),"{error}");
}

#[test]
fn backfill_source_expiry_applies_cascade_set_null_and_restrict_atomically() {
 for action in ["cascade","set null","restrict"] {for restored in [false,true] {
  let optional=if action=="set null" {" ?"}else{""};
  let old=format!("table parents:\n  until: time\n  expire at .until\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable children:\n  parent: parents{optional} on delete {action}\n  cap 4 by parent\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");
  let next=old.replace("table items:\n  n:","table items:\n  doubled: int\n  n:")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
  let(s,p)=compile(&old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
  let rows=[("parents",json!({"id":"p","until":50})),("children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"})),("items",json!({"id":"i","n":3}))];
  let rows=rows.into_iter().map(|(t,r)|(t.to_owned(),wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables[t],&r).unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>();e.seed_at(rows,0).unwrap();
  if restored {let(s,p)=compile("table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,1).unwrap();}
  let g=e.generation();let(s,p)=compile(&next);let result=e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(3,Some(if restored {2}else{1})),false,100);
  if action=="restrict" {let error=result.unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("source expiry")&&error.contains("restrict"),"{error}");assert_eq!(g,e.generation());assert!(row(&mut e,"items","i").field("doubled").is_err());}
  else {result.unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("parents").unwrap(),0);if action=="cascade" {assert_eq!(e.store().count("children").unwrap(),0);}else{assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"children","c","parent"),Value::Null);}assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","i","doubled"),Value::Int(6));}
 }}
}
#[test]
fn reset_rebuilds_retained_schema_metadata_before_native_inserts_or_reseeding() {
 for reseed in [false,true] {
  let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
  let next=old.replace("  n: int","  derived: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {derived:n * 2}\n";
  let mut e=engine(old);adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
  let native=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["items"],&json!({"id":"native","n":3,"derived":9})).unwrap();
  e.dev_reset_at(if reseed {vec![("items".into(),native)]}else{vec![]},crate::now_ms()).unwrap();
  if !reseed {seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"native","n":3,"derived":9}));}
  adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","native","derived"),Value::Int(9));
 }
}

#[test]
fn enum_replacement_accepts_original_preimages_and_preserves_native_outputs() {
 let old="table cues:\n  status: enum('standby','done')\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let second=old.replace("enum('standby','done')","enum('standby','complete','skipped')")+"backfill cues complete(status: enum('standby','done')):\n  {status:'complete' if status = 'done' else status}\n";
 let third=old.replace("enum('standby','done')","enum('standby','finished','skipped')")+"backfill cues finished(status: enum('standby','done','complete','skipped')):\n  {status:'finished' if status = 'done' or status = 'complete' else status}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"cues",json!({"id":"a","status":"done"}));adopt(&mut e,&second,2).unwrap();seed(&mut e,"cues",json!({"id":"native","status":"complete"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&third,3).unwrap();for id in ["a","native"]{assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"cues",id,"status"),Value::String("finished".into()));}
 adopt(&mut e,&second,2).unwrap();for id in ["a","native"]{assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"cues",id,"status"),Value::String("complete".into()));}
}

#[test]
fn retained_hidden_optional_inputs_are_used_when_reentering_a_backfill() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int ?\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next="table items:\n  derived: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nbackfill items twice(n: int ?):\n  {derived:(n ?? 0) * 2}\n";
 let bridge="table items:\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","derived"),Value::Int(6));
 adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,bridge,3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","derived"),Value::Int(6));
}
#[test]
fn source_expiry_observes_children_removed_in_the_same_release() {
 for action in ["cascade","set null","restrict"] {
  let optional=if action=="set null" {" ?"}else{""};
  let parent="table parents:\n  until: time\n  expire at .until\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
  let child=format!("table children:\n  parent: parents{optional} on delete {action}\n  cap 4 by parent\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");
  let item="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
  let old=format!("{parent}{child}{item}");let next=format!("{parent}{}backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {{doubled:n * 2}}\n",item.replace("  n:","  doubled: int\n  n:"));
  let(s,p)=compile(&old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
  let rows=[("parents",json!({"id":"p","until":50})),("children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"})),("items",json!({"id":"i","n":3}))];
  let rows=rows.into_iter().map(|(t,r)|(t.to_owned(),wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables[t],&r).unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>();e.seed_at(rows,0).unwrap();
  let(s,p)=compile(&next);let result=e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,100);
  if action=="restrict" {let error=result.unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("restrict"),"{error}");assert_eq!(e.store().count("parents").unwrap(),1);}else{
   result.unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("parents").unwrap(),0);
   let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let t=tx.num("children").unwrap();
   let saved:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id='c'",[t],|r|r.get(0)).optional().unwrap();
   if action=="cascade" {assert!(saved.is_none());}else{let saved:Value=serde_json::from_str(&saved.unwrap()).unwrap();assert_eq!(*saved.field("parent").unwrap(),Value::Null);}
  }
 }
}

#[test]
fn physically_sparse_defaults_are_interpreted_and_receipts_keep_that_interpretation() {
 for kind in ["int","int ?"] {
  let old="table items:\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
  let defaulted=old.replace("  public","  n: int default 3\n  public");
  let fill=format!("backfill items twice(n: {kind}):\n  {{doubled:(n ?? 0) * 2}}\n");
  let next=defaulted.replace("  n:","  doubled: int\n  n:")+&fill;
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a"}));adopt(&mut e,&defaulted,2).unwrap();
  assert!(row(&mut e,"items","a").field("n").is_err(),"fixture is physically sparse");
  adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(6));
  let later=next.replace("default 3","default 100");adopt(&mut e,&later,4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",5).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&later,4).unwrap();
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(6));
  let rollback=later.replace("  doubled: int\n","").replace(&fill,"");adopt(&mut e,&rollback,6).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&later,4).unwrap();
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(6),"rollback/forward keeps the original omitted-input interpretation");
 }
}

#[test]
fn saved_generic_id_binding_survives_type_erasure_and_multiple_restorations() {
 let parents="table parents:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let old=parents.to_owned()+"table children:\n  parent: parents on delete keep\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  parent:","  copied: int\n  parent:")+"backfill children copy(parent: id):\n  {copied:(parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
 let erased=next.replace("parent: parents on delete keep","parent: id");
 let mut e=engine(&old);seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":3}));seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&erased,3).unwrap();
 adopt(&mut e,parents,4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&erased,3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,parents,4).unwrap();
 let table=e.schema().tables["parents"].clone();{let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let r=tx.get("parents","p").unwrap().unwrap();tx.delete("parents",&table,&r).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}
 seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":77}));let error=adopt(&mut e,&erased,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("parent lifetime"),"{error}");
}

#[test]
fn reentering_omission_cohorts_do_not_change_a_new_declarations_source_snapshot() {
 let old="table items:\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let defaulted=old.replace("  public","  n: int default 3\n  public");
 let fill="backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let next=defaulted.replace("  n:","  doubled: int\n  n:")+fill;
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a"}));adopt(&mut e,&defaulted,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();
 let rollback=defaulted.replace("default 3","default 100");adopt(&mut e,&rollback,4).unwrap();
 let fresh=next.replace("default 3","default 100").replace("  doubled:","  fresh: int\n  doubled:")+"backfill items current(n: int):\n  {fresh:n * 2}\n";
 adopt(&mut e,&fresh,5).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(6));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","fresh"),Value::Int(200));
}

#[test]
fn set_null_expiry_updates_a_column_archived_during_that_release() {
 let parent="table parents:\n  until: time\n  expire at .until\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let child="table children:\n  parent: parents ? on delete set null\n  cap 4 by parent\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let item="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let old=format!("{parent}{child}{item}");let next=old.replace("  parent: parents ? on delete set null\n","").replace("  cap 4 by parent\n","").replace("  n:","  doubled: int\n  n:")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let(s,p)=compile(&old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
 let rows=[("parents",json!({"id":"p","until":50})),("children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"})),("items",json!({"id":"i","n":3}))];
 let rows=rows.into_iter().map(|(t,r)|(t.to_owned(),wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables[t],&r).unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>();e.seed_at(rows,0).unwrap();
 let(s,p)=compile(&next);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,100).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("parents").unwrap(),0);
 let fresh=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["parents"],&json!({"id":"p","until":500})).unwrap();e.seed_at([("parents".into(),fresh)],100).unwrap();
 let(s,p)=compile(&old);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,Some(2)),false,101).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"children","c","parent"),Value::Null);
}

#[test]
fn historical_lifetime_outputs_refuse_before_transforming_or_retiring_children() {
 for action in ["cascade","set null","restrict"] {
  let optional=if action=="set null" {" ?"}else{""};
  let old=format!("table parents:\n  until: time\n  expire at .until\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable children:\n  parent: parents{optional} on delete {action}\n  cap 4 by parent\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");
  let next=old.replace("  expire at .until\n","")+"backfill parents shorten(until: time):\n  {until:until - 1d}\n";
  let(s,p)=compile(&old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
  let rows=[("parents",json!({"id":"p","until":500})),("children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"}))];let rows=rows.into_iter().map(|(t,r)|(t.to_owned(),wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables[t],&r).unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>();e.seed_at(rows,0).unwrap();
  let generation=e.generation();let(s,p)=compile(&next);let error=e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,100).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical lifetime"),"{error}");assert_eq!(e.generation(),generation);assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"parents","p","until"),Value::Int(500));assert_eq!(e.store().count("children").unwrap(),1);
 }
}

 for (kind,value,reason) in [("text 2..30","' '","non-whitespace"),("text 2..30 format handle","'Not A Handle'","handle"),("text <=80 format email","'bad@@example.test'","email")] {
  let next=old.replace("  n:",&format!("  label: {kind}\n  n:"))+&format!("backfill items label():\n  {{label:{value}}}\n");
  let mut e=engine(old);for id in ["a","b"]{seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":id,"n":3}));}let generation=e.generation();let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains(reason)&&error.contains("2 affected rows"),"{error}");assert_eq!(e.generation(),generation);assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),2);assert!(row(&mut e,"items","a").field("label").is_err());
 }
}

#[test]
fn widened_narrowed_rows_capture_their_original_reference_argument_types() {
 let parents="table parents:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let old=parents.to_owned()+"table children:\n  parent: parents on delete keep\n  n: int 0..10\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let narrow=old.replace("parent: parents on delete keep","parent: id").replace("int 0..10","int 0..5");
 let wide=narrow.replace("int 0..5","int 0..10").replace("  parent: id","  copied: int\n  parent: id")+"backfill children copy(parent: id):\n  {copied:(parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
 let mut e=engine(&old);seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":3}));seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p","n":9}));adopt(&mut e,&narrow,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("children").unwrap(),0);adopt(&mut e,&wide,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"children","c","copied"),Value::Int(3));
 adopt(&mut e,parents,4).unwrap();let table=e.schema().tables["parents"].clone();{let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let r=tx.get("parents","p").unwrap().unwrap();tx.delete("parents",&table,&r).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":77}));
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&wide,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("parent lifetime"),"{error}");
}
#[test]
fn widened_narrowed_rows_cannot_rewrite_their_original_lifetime_fields() {
 for (clause,field,value) in [("expire at .until","until",500),("not before .opens","opens",0)] {
  let old=format!("table items:\n  n: int 0..10\n  {field}: time\n  {clause}\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");
  let narrow=old.replace("int 0..10","int 0..5").replace(&format!("  {clause}\n"),"");
  let next=narrow.replace("int 0..5","int 0..10")+&format!("backfill items move({field}: time):\n  {{{field}:{field} + 1d}}\n");
  let(s,p)=compile(&old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
  let input=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["items"],&json!({"id":"a","n":9,field:value})).unwrap();e.seed_at([("items".into(),input)],0).unwrap();
  let(s,p)=compile(&narrow);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0);
  let(s,p)=compile(&next);let error=e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(3,Some(2)),false,2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical lifetime"),"{error}");assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0);
 }
}

fn returning_narrowed_rows_interpret_columns_added_by_later_predecessors() {

}

#[test]
fn restored_output_cells_keep_preimages_across_rollback_and_table_suspension() {
 for kind in ["int","int ?"] {
  let old=format!("table items:\n  n: int\n  score: {kind}\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");
  let dropped=old.replace(&format!("  score: {kind}\n"),"");let next=old.clone()+"backfill items score(n: int):\n  {score:n * 2}\n";
  let mut e=engine(&old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3,"score":7}));adopt(&mut e,&dropped,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(6));
  adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(7));adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(7));adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(6));
 }
}
#[test]
fn replacing_added_output_mappings_restores_each_version_instead_of_a_default() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let twice=old.replace("  n:","  score: int default 0\n  n:")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {score:n * 2}\n";let triple=twice.replace("twice(n:","triple(n:").replace("n * 2","n * 3");
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&triple,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(9));
 adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(6));adopt(&mut e,&triple,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(9));
}

#[test]

//! A process-local clock for development and explicitly classified staging stores.
use snapback4_core::error::Refusal;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

const MAX_TIME: i64 = snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX;

pub fn input(message: impl Into<String>) -> Refusal { Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INPUT", "clock", message).guide("clock") }
pub fn release() -> Refusal { Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_DEV_ONLY", "clock", "clock controls require dev or a deploy --staging store; production always uses wall time").guide("clock") }

/// Isolated time tests stay inside an assigned range, or request a free port.
pub(crate) fn isolated_command() -> String {
    isolated_range(std::env::var("PORT_LO").ok().as_deref(), std::env::var("PORT_HI").ok().as_deref())
}
fn isolated_range(lo: Option<&str>, hi: Option<&str>) -> String {
    match (lo.and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok()), hi.and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok())) {
        (Some(lo), Some(hi)) if lo > 0 && hi >= lo => format!("dev --memory --port {lo}-{hi} within the current range"),
        _ => "dev --memory --port 0".into(),
    }
}

pub fn duration(text: &str) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    if text.starts_with('-') { return Err(input("clock advance cannot move backwards; use a positive duration such as +2m")); }
    let text = text.strip_prefix('+').unwrap_or(text);
    let split = text.bytes().position(|b| !b.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(text.len());
    let (number, unit) = text.split_at(split);
    let multiplier = match unit { "ms" => 1, "s" => 1_000, "m" => 60_000, "h" => 3_600_000, "d" => 86_400_000, "w" => 604_800_000, _ => 0 };
    number.parse::<i64>().ok().and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(multiplier)).filter(|n| *n > 0 && *n <= MAX_TIME)
        .ok_or_else(|| input("expected a positive whole duration with ms, s, m, h, d or w, such as +25h"))
}

pub fn offset(text: &str) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    if let Some(rest) = text.strip_prefix('-') { duration(rest).map(|n| -n) } else { duration(text) }
}

fn time(value: Option<i64>) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    value.filter(|n| (0..=MAX_TIME).contains(n)).ok_or_else(|| input("time must be Unix milliseconds in 0..9007199254740991; choose a smaller time or duration"))
}

pub fn run_now(text: Option<&String>) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    let now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    match text {
        None => Ok(now),
        Some(text) if text.starts_with('+') => time(now.checked_add(duration(text)?)),
        Some(text) => time(text.parse().ok()),
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Clock {
    dev: bool,
    base: i64,
    offset: i64,
    started: Instant,
    epoch: String,
    revision: u64,
    #[cfg(test)]
    frozen: bool,
}

impl Clock {
    pub fn new(dev: bool, offset: i64) -> Result<Self, Refusal> {
        if !dev && offset != 0 { return Err(release()); }
        Ok(Self { dev, offset, base: time(snapback4_core::now_ms().checked_add(offset))?, started: Instant::now(),
            epoch: snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?, revision: 0, #[cfg(test)] frozen: false })
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(crate) fn freeze(&mut self, now: i64) { self.base = now; self.frozen = true; }
    pub fn now(&self) -> i64 {
        #[cfg(test)] if self.frozen { return self.base; }
        if !self.dev { return snapback4_core::now_ms(); }
        self.base.saturating_add(self.started.elapsed().as_millis().min(MAX_TIME as u128) as i64).min(MAX_TIME)
    }
    /// Identifies samples taken before/after a restart or deliberate clock jump.
    pub fn offset(&self) -> i64 { self.offset }
    pub fn revision(&self) -> String { format!("{}:{}", self.epoch, self.revision) }
    pub fn presence_now(&self) -> i64 {
        // Production grace is elapsed real time, unaffected by wall-clock corrections.
        if self.dev { self.now() } else { self.started.elapsed().as_millis().min(MAX_TIME as u128) as i64 }
    }
    /// Back to wall time: the offset `--clock-offset` and every advance added
    /// is dropped (a development reset's `{"clock":"reset"}`).
    pub fn reset(&mut self) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
        if !self.dev { return Err(release()); }
        self.revision += 1;
        self.offset = 0;
        self.base = snapback4_core::now_ms();
        self.started = Instant::now();
        #[cfg(test)] { self.frozen = false; }
        Ok(self.base)
    }
    pub fn advance(&mut self, milliseconds: i64) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
        if !self.dev { return Err(release()); }
        if milliseconds <= 0 { return Err(input("clock advance cannot move backwards; use a positive duration such as +2m")); }
        let next = time(self.now().checked_add(milliseconds))?;
        let offset = self.offset.checked_add(milliseconds).ok_or_else(|| input("cumulative clock offset overflow"))?;
        self.revision += 1;
        self.offset = offset;
        self.base = next;
        self.started = Instant::now();
        Ok(next)
    }
}

/// The control CLI only talks to a loopback dev host. It does not open its store.
pub fn command(options: &crate::verbs::Options) -> i32 {
    let result = (|| -> Result<String, String> {
        if options.positionals.len() != 2 || options.positionals[0] != "advance" {
            return Err(input(format!("usage: {}", crate::cli_flags::synopsis("clock"))).to_string());
        }
        duration(&options.positionals[1]).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let release_path = options.flags.get("release").map(|dir| std::path::PathBuf::from(dir).join("store.sqlite"));
        if release_path.is_some() && options.flags.contains_key("port") { return Err("clock --release does not accept --port".into()); }
        let (address, instance, token) = if let Some(path) = &release_path {
            let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            if store.meta("deployment_environment").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(release().to_string()); }
            let address = crate::store_owner::inspector_address(path).ok_or("staging clock requires a running serve")?;
            let session = crate::serve::token::mint_live(path, "clock", 1).map_err(|(_,error)|error)?;
            (address,None,Some(session.token))
        } else { let (address,instance)=crate::verbs::dev_address(options)?; (address,instance,None) };
        let body = serde_json::json!({"duration":options.positionals[1],"eachBoundary":options.flags.contains_key("each-boundary")}).to_string();
        // The common HTTP/1.0 exchange is EOF-delimited and bounds replies at
        // 64 MiB, enough for the preflight's complete explicit window report.
        let response = if let Some(token) = &token {
            crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange_authorized(address,"POST","/dev/clock/advance",None,Some(token),&body,Duration::from_secs(60))
        } else { crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange_dev(address, "/dev/clock/advance", instance.as_deref(), &body, Duration::from_secs(60)) };
        let (status, json) = response.map_err(|error| {
            let evidence = crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange(address, "GET", "/dev/status", None, "", Duration::from_secs(5)).ok()
                .map(|(_, status)| format!("now={}, skewMs={}", status["now"], status["skewMs"]))
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "clock status unavailable".into());
            format!("clock advance reply failed at {address}: {error}; time may already have advanced ({evidence}); inspect /dev/status and queued work before retrying; for an isolated dev pass --port N")
        })?;
        if let Some(denied) = json.get("denied") {
            let clock = json["message"].as_str().map(|message| format!("; time remains advanced: {message}")).unwrap_or_default();
            return Err(format!("{}: {}{clock}\n  guide: clock", denied["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("E_CLOCK_INPUT"), denied["message"].as_str().unwrap_or("clock advance failed")));
        }
        if status != 200 { return Err(format!("dev refused clock advance: {json}")); }
        Ok(json.to_string())
    })();
    match result { Ok(body) => { println!("{body}"); 0 }, Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); 1 } }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod isolated_tests {
    #[test]
    fn time_test_guidance_uses_only_valid_assigned_ranges_or_a_free_port() {
        assert_eq!(super::isolated_range(Some("24000"), Some("24009")), "dev --memory --port 24000-24009 within the current range");
        for (lo,hi) in [(None,None),(Some("0"),Some("3")),(Some("4"),Some("3")),(Some("bad"),Some("3"))] {
            assert_eq!(super::isolated_range(lo,hi), "dev --memory --port 0");
        }
    }
}

//! Server activation builder; all fallible work precedes commit.
use super::*;
impl Engine {
    pub(super) fn activate(
        store: &mut Store,
        schema: &Schema,
        programs: &BTreeMap<String, Program>,
        release: Option<&release::ReleaseActivation>,
        now: i64,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
        expected_release: Option<u64>,
        expected_backend: Option<&crate::store::opening::Backend>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, crate::store::opening::Metadata), RuntimeError> {
        schema.validate_column_owners()?;
        for program in programs.values() {
            crate::schema::ephemeral::admit_program(schema, program)?;
            admission::check_state_predicates(schema, program)?;
        }
        for program in programs.values() {
            for table in crate::ir::read_sites(program)
                .into_iter()
                .map(|s| s.table)
                .chain(crate::ir::write_tables(program).into_iter().map(|(t, _)| t))
            {
                schema.refuse_private(&table)?;
            }
        }
        for schedule in &schema.schedules {
            if !schedule.valid()
            {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
                    "schedule {} has an invalid period or offset",
                    schedule.name
                )));
            }
            let Some(program) = programs
                .get(&schedule.target)
                .filter(|p| matches!(p.kind, ProgramKind::Job | ProgramKind::Action))
            else {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
                    "schedule {} names missing job or action {}; restore or remove the schedule",
                    schedule.name, schedule.target
                )));
            };
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action {
                let seat = schedule
                    .service
                    .as_deref()
                    .filter(|seat| crate::identity::is_service(seat))
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        RuntimeError::Schema(
                            "a scheduled action requires its declared service seat".into(),
                        )
                    })?;
                actions::authorize(program, seat)?;
            } else if schedule.service.is_some() {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                    "a scheduled job cannot name a service seat".into(),
                ));
            }
            if program.args.iter().any(|(name, kind)| {
                !matches!(name.as_str(), "periodStart" | "periodEnd")
                    || *kind != crate::ir::ArgType::Time
            }) {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                    "scheduled work takes periodStart: time and periodEnd: time, or nothing".into(),
                ));
            }

    // Read tokens and malformed credentials always enter this guard. Otherwise
    // authenticated full-token handlers retain their input/refusal ordering.
    // Public and dev doors must resolve any supplied credential before dispatch.

        else { respond_timed(request,shared,status,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)})); }

    if let Err(refusal) = dev_request(&request, shared) {

    /// Remove a row the engine itself retires (an expired one): no rule and
    /// no constraint stands between the engine and its own sweep, while
    /// aggregates and partition events apply as for any delete.
    pub(super) fn remove(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: &Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.refuse_engine_owned(table_name, Effect::Delete)?;
        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
        let entries = self.maintain_scratch(self.contribution_heap(table), |runner| {
            runner.tx.delete(table_name, table, old)
        })?;
        self.charge_source_indexes(table, Some(old), None, entries)?;
        self.row_changed(table_name, Some(old), None, Origin::Source)
    }

    /// Physically retire one row whose upper lifetime ended. Expiry bypasses
    /// the root's authored delete rule and constraints, but it does not bypass
    /// the schema's declared effects on rows that reference the root. A
    /// read-through child still retains the expired parent physically.
    // @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — expiry cleanup runs the same closure/restrict/set-null actions as an authored delete.
    pub(crate) fn retire_expired_row(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if self.physical_children(table_name, old.field("id")?)? { return Ok(false); }
        let current = if self.schema.is_referenced(table_name) {
            self.apply_reference_actions(table_name, old)?;
            // A cascade may maintain a column on the root; delete its current
            // physical image so index and cap entries match what is stored.
            let id = old.field("id")?.as_str()?;
            self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, id))?
                .unwrap_or_else(|| old.clone())
        } else {
            old.clone()
        };
        self.remove(table_name, &current)?;
        Ok(true)
    }

    /// At a program commit's end, retire every source row the commit changed
    /// or inserted whose lifetime has already ended: its final
    /// image was never live at a commit boundary, so it leaves no row for a
    /// later sweep to announce. Inside the commit the
    /// row existed as written (an insert-then-update still works). Engine
    /// bookkeeping, unmetered: the program's metering is already recorded.
    /// Server execution and device prediction both run it so a predicted
    /// final image cannot omit cascade, set-null or restrict effects. Its work,
    /// returned, stays
    /// linear in the rows retired: each aggregate row takes one net change
    /// and each audience key is scanned for a live row once. Seeds and loads
    /// ordinarily keep their rows for the sweep; row-keyed view sources use
    /// `retire_seeded_view_sources` to preserve their count-basis contract.
    // @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — a row whose lifetime ended by its own commit's end never exists at a commit boundary.
    pub(crate) fn retire_expired_births(&mut self) -> Result<crate::meter::Metered, RuntimeError> {

    fn activate_transaction_with<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, check: impl FnOnce(&Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, R), RuntimeError> {
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — a removed opening boundary cannot lower durable source retirement time.
        let now=if self.server {now.max(self.meta("time_audience_clock")?.and_then(|s|s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(i64::MIN))}else{now};
        super::live_activation::configure(&self.conn,schema)?;
        let mut report = super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::default();
        let mut phase = std::time::Instant::now();
        if programs.is_some() {
            if let Some(estimate) = self.adoption_estimate(schema)?.filter(|e| e.seconds().1 > 1.0) {
                crate::diagnostic!("snapback4: atomic adoption: {}", estimate.summary());
            }
            let estimates = self.check_timeline_activation_at(schema, now, allow_launch)?;
            for estimate in estimates.iter().filter(|e| e.sources != 0) { crate::diagnostic!("snapback4: atomic feed adoption: {}", estimate.summary()); }
        }
        report.phase("read-only advice", &mut phase);
        self.check_ownership()?;
        let sql = self.conn.transaction_with_behavior(rusqlite::TransactionBehavior::Immediate)?;
        // Low-level preparation may precede a writer-lock wait. Recheck all
        // opening admission under the lock that owns the upgrades.
        Self::check_version(&sql)?;
        let before = identity(&sql)?;
        if before != self.identity { return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("another engine activated this store; reopen it before activating source".into())); }
        check(&sql)?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — physical upgrades roll back with adoption.
        // Rollback restores schema/rows and freelist count. It may leave
        // changed free-page contents or journal/WAL residue, not committed data.
        Self::initialize_schema(&sql)?;
        let now=if self.server {
            now.max(sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='time_audience_clock'",[],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0)).optional()?.and_then(|s|s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(i64::MIN))
        }else{now};
        if programs.is_some() { Self::initialize_change_evidence(&sql)?; }
        index_key_guards(&sql)?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — reuse an admitted key bound, never assume a legacy store was checked.
        // Physical INSERT/UPDATE triggers invalidate the proof on oversized keys,
        // including direct SQL. Every activation key
        // below is checked too. Bind the proof to the predecessor so an older
        // binary's adoption invalidates it; device installation always scans.
        let key_proof = before.1.as_ref().map(|hash| format!("{}:{hash}", crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES));
        let validated: Option<String> = sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'index_keys_validated'", [], |r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
        let keys_validated = programs.is_some() && key_proof.is_some() && validated == key_proof;
        #[cfg(test)]
        let keys_validated = keys_validated && !self.full_adoption;
        // One bounded result, no row images. Fresh stores have no schema;
        // an explicit device reset discards the old entries in this transaction.
        if before.0.is_some() && !reset && !keys_validated {
            let violation: Option<(String, i64)> = sql.query_row(
                "SELECT c.name, COUNT(*) FROM i JOIN _catalog c ON c.num = i.ti WHERE length(i.k) > ?1 GROUP BY i.ti ORDER BY i.ti LIMIT 1",
                [crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as i64], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?))
            ).optional()?;
            if let Some((name, count)) = violation {
                let (table, index) = name.rsplit_once('.').unwrap_or((&name, "unknown"));
                return Err(crate::rows::index_key_refusal(table, index, count as u64));
            }
        }
        report.phase("key validation", &mut phase);
        let schema_hash = schema.hash();
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#6-devices-and-partitions — executable native content changes the existing semantic generation.
        let native: Option<String> = sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release_native_digest'", [], |r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
        let activation_hash = programs.map(|programs| match &native {Some(native)=>digest(&(schema,programs,native)),None=>digest(&(schema,programs))});
        let schema_changed = before.0.as_deref() != Some(&schema_hash);
        let mut changed = schema_changed || activation_hash.as_ref().is_some_and(|hash| before.1.as_ref() != Some(hash));
        // The address function is part of the semantic generation even when
        // the authored schema and programs are byte-identical. Devices reset
        // with that generation; the server rebuilds old target rows below.
        let predecessor_has_aggregate = sql.query_row(
            "SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation = (SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation')",
            [], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0),
        ).optional()?.map(|text| serde_json::from_str::<Schema>(&text)
            .map_err(|error| RuntimeError::decode(error, RuntimeError::Store))).transpose()?
            .is_some_and(|previous| previous.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none()));
        let maintained_identity_upgrade = programs.is_some()
            && predecessor_has_aggregate
            && sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key=?1", [crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.as_deref()
                != Some(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION);
        changed |= maintained_identity_upgrade;
        let mut catalog: BTreeMap<String, i64> = sql.prepare("SELECT name, num FROM _catalog")?
            .query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
        // Repeat source-count admission under the writer lock, before any candidate writes.
        let preflight_catalog = catalog.clone();
        let (launches, sql) = {
            let preflight = Tx { activation_scan: None, changes: Default::default(), sequence_table: Default::default(), tx: sql, page_size: self.page_size,
                server: self.server,
                catalog: &preflight_catalog };
            let launches = if programs.is_some() { super::timeline_launch::preflight(&preflight, schema, now, allow_launch)? } else { Vec::new() };
            let Tx { tx: sql, .. } = preflight;
            (launches, sql)
        };
        let mut next = catalog.values().copied().max().unwrap_or(0) + 1;
        for (name, table) in schema.tables.iter().filter(|(_,t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
            for full in std::iter::once(name.clone()).chain(table.indexes.keys().map(|index| format!("{name}.{index}"))) {
                if !catalog.contains_key(&full) {
                    sql.execute("INSERT INTO _catalog(name, num) VALUES (?1, ?2)", params![full, next])?;
                    catalog.insert(full, next); next += 1;
                }
            }
        }
        let activation_scan = programs.map(|_| Default::default());
        #[cfg(test)]
        let activation_scan = if self.full_adoption { None } else { activation_scan };
        let tx = Tx { activation_scan, changes: Default::default(), sequence_table: Default::default(), tx: sql, page_size: self.page_size,
            server: self.server,
            catalog: &catalog };
        let now = if schema.tables.values().any(|table| table.not_before.is_some()) {
            tx.pin_time_audience_clock(now)?
        } else {
            now
        };
        // @ref LLP 3000.013#12.1 — occupancy belongs to successful activation,
        // never opening a legacy store that may subsequently refuse adoption.
        let cap_counts = crate::rows::caps::required(schema) || tx.meta("cap_counts_schema")?.is_some();
        if cap_counts {
            tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _cap_counts (t INTEGER NOT NULL, k BLOB NOT NULL, n INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(n >= 0), PRIMARY KEY(t, k)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
            if reset { tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _cap_counts;")?; }
        }
        if schema.tables.values().any(|t| !t.sequences.is_empty()) {
            tx.ensure_sequence_table()?;
        }
        if reset {
            tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM r; DELETE FROM i;")?;
            if tx.has_sequence_table()? { tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _sequences;")?; }
        }
        super::live_activation::clear_stale(&tx)?;
        let mut retained_through=BTreeSet::new();
        let release_activation=programs.is_some() && tx.meta("activation_restore")?.is_some();
        if (schema_changed || release_activation) && programs.is_some() && !reset {
            let previous: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')", [], |r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
            let previous: Option<Schema> = previous.map(|s|serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))).transpose()?;
            let restore=tx.meta("activation_restore")?.as_deref()!=Some("false");
            retained_through=super::dormant::transition(&tx,schema,previous.as_ref(),restore,now,&mut report)?;
        }
        let population_changed=tx.meta("activation_population")?.as_deref()==Some("true");
        changed |= population_changed;
        let rebuilding=schema_changed || population_changed;
        if rebuilding && programs.is_some() && !reset {
            let started = std::time::Instant::now();
            let previous: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation = (SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation')", [], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
            let previous: Option<Schema> = previous.map(|s| {
                    serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::decode(e,RuntimeError::Store))
                }).transpose()?;
            let shortened = crate::engine::through_activation::activate_retained(&tx,schema,previous.as_ref(),now,&retained_through)?;
            tx.set_meta("read_through_shortened",&canonical_json(&shortened))?;
            report.detail("read-through retirement", started);
        }
        if programs.is_some() { crate::engine::timeline::check_activation(&tx, schema)?; }
        if rebuilding && programs.is_some() {
            let started = std::time::Instant::now();
            super::timeline_launch::build(&tx, schema, &launches, now)?;
            report.detail("feed launch", started);
            let started = std::time::Instant::now();
            super::event_slots::fill(&tx, schema, now)?;

//! The CLI's local service-seat door. This capability never comes from a bearer session.
use super::{Shared, single_header, authority_host, producing_engine, respond_with_cors};
use snapback4_core::{error::Refusal, identity::{self, Session}};
use std::{io::{self, Read, Seek, SeekFrom, Write}, net::TcpStream, path::{Path, PathBuf}, time::Duration};
use serde_json::{Value as Json, json};
use tiny_http::{Method, Request};

pub(crate) const ENDPOINT: &str = "/dev/token";
#[path = "serve_token_protocol.rs"]
mod protocol;
use protocol::{Mint, CLIENT, SERVER, PROOF};
pub(crate) use protocol::Authority;
const BODY_LIMIT: usize = 4096;
const SECRET_LEN: usize = 64;

fn secret_path(store: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    let mut path = store.as_os_str().to_owned();
    path.push("-token");
    path.into()
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — preserve the hardened auxiliary-file checks and the single owner's lifetime.
pub(crate) fn publish(store: &Path) -> io::Result<Authority> {
    #[cfg(unix)] {
        let secret = protocol::hex(&protocol::random()?);
        let mut file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), true, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
        file.set_len(0)?;
        file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(0))?;
        file.write_all(secret.as_bytes())?;
        Ok(Authority::new(secret, protocol::store_identity(store)?))
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
        let _ = store;
        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    }
}

fn read_secret(store: &Path) -> io::Result<String> {
    #[cfg(unix)] {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), false, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
        if file.metadata()?.permissions().mode() & 0o777 != 0o600 {
            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof must have mode 0600"));
        }
        let mut secret = String::new();
        file.take((SECRET_LEN + 1) as u64).read_to_string(&mut secret)?;
        if secret.len() != SECRET_LEN || !secret.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof is incomplete"));
        }
        Ok(secret)
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
        let _ = store;
        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    }
}

fn refusal(code: &str, family: &str, message: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Refusal {
    Refusal::new(code, family, message).site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(rewrite).guide("services")
}

pub(super) fn denied(request: Request) {
    respond_with_cors(request, 403, json!({"denied": refusal("E_AUTH", "auth",
        "service token minting requires a loopback peer and this server's private store proof",
        "run snapback4 token --service <name> on the server host as the store owner (add --release <dir> for serve)")}), false);
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — authenticate both ends before minting; the store key never leaves its host file/memory.
pub(super) fn handle(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    let local = request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|peer| peer.ip().is_loopback())
        && matches!(single_header(&request, "host"), Ok(Some(host)) if authority_host(host)
            .is_some_and(|host| host.parse::<std::net::IpAddr>().is_ok_and(|ip| ip.is_loopback())))
        && matches!(single_header(&request, "origin"), Ok(None))
        && matches!(single_header(&request, "x-snapback-store-secret"), Ok(None));
    let Some(authority) = shared.owner.as_ref().and_then(|o| o.token_authority.as_ref()).filter(|_| local) else {
        return denied(request);
    };
    if request.method() != &Method::Post || request.url() != ENDPOINT
        || request.body_length().is_some_and(|n| n > BODY_LIMIT) { return denied(request); }
    let client = match single_header(&request, CLIENT) {
        Ok(Some(value)) => match protocol::nonce(value) { Some(nonce) => nonce, None => return denied(request) },
        _ => return denied(request),
    };
    let (server, proof) = match (single_header(&request, SERVER), single_header(&request, PROOF)) {
        (Ok(None), Ok(None)) => (None, None),
        (Ok(Some(server)), Ok(Some(proof))) => match protocol::nonce(server) {
            Some(server) => (Some(server), Some(proof.to_owned())), None => return denied(request),
        },
        _ => return denied(request),
    };
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    if request.as_reader().take((BODY_LIMIT + 1) as u64).read_to_end(&mut bytes).is_err() || bytes.len() > BODY_LIMIT {
        return denied(request);
    }
    let mint: Mint = match serde_json::from_slice::<Mint>(&bytes) {
        Ok(mint) if mint.store == authority.store => mint,
        _ => return denied(request),
    };
    let binding = mint.binding();
    let Some(server) = server else {
        let Some(server) = authority.challenge(client, binding.clone(), std::time::Instant::now()) else { return denied(request); };
        return respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"server_nonce": protocol::hex(&server),
            "server_proof": protocol::server_proof(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding)}), false);
    };
    if !authority.consume(&client, &server, &binding, proof.as_deref().unwrap(), std::time::Instant::now()) {
        return denied(request);
    }
    let result = {
        // Use the same writer and identity routine as offline minting. Sample
        // wall time as the offline CLI does, independent of dev's test clock.
        let engine = producing_engine(shared);
        shared.owner.as_ref().expect("authorized owner").path().and_then(|_| {
            let now = if engine.store().meta("deployment_environment")?.as_deref() == Some("staging") { shared.now() } else { snapback4_core::now_ms() };
            identity::service_session(engine.store(), &mint.service, now, mint.days)
        })
    };
    let reply = match result {
        Ok(session) => json!({"session": session}),
        Err(error) => {
            let why = error.refusal().site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(if matches!(error, snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth(_)) {
                "use --service with 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -"
            } else { "inspect the store error, repair the store access, then rerun snapback4 token" }).guide("services");
            json!({"denied": why})
        }
    };
    let Some(sealed) = protocol::seal(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding, &serde_json::to_vec(&reply).unwrap()) else {
        return denied(request);
    };
    respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"sealed": sealed}), false);
}

fn exchange(address: std::net::SocketAddr, headers: &str, body: &str) -> io::Result<Json> {
    let mut stream = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, Duration::from_secs(1))?;
    stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10)))?;
    stream.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(2)))?;
    // HTTP/1.0 keeps the bounded reply length-delimited. No proxy, DNS or redirects.
    write!(stream, "POST {ENDPOINT} HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: {address}\r\n{headers}Content-Type: application/json\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len())?;
    let mut response = String::new();
    stream.take(8193).read_to_string(&mut response)?;
    response.split_once("\r\n\r\n").filter(|_| response.len() <= 8192
        && (response.starts_with("HTTP/1.0 200 ") || response.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 200 ")))
        .and_then(|(_, body)| serde_json::from_str(body).ok())
        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::other("invalid local mint reply"))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — busy token commands ask the announced loopback owner, never open a second writer.
pub(crate) fn mint_live(path: &Path, name: &str, days: i64) -> Result<Session, (i32, String)> {
    let store = path.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_owned());
    let owner = crate::store_owner::address(&store).unwrap_or_else(|| "another CLI command or a server starting up (address pending)".into());
    let unavailable = |reason: &str| (2, format!("E_STORE_BUSY at {}: owned by {owner}; {reason}. Restore access to that server's loopback listener and private store proof, then rerun `snapback4 token --service <name>` (with the same --release <dir> for serve). For an older or unreachable server, stop it, rerun token offline, then restart it; for another CLI, wait for it to finish. Leave <database>-owner in place", path.display()));
    let address = crate::store_owner::inspector_address(&store).ok_or_else(|| unavailable("no published loopback listener; no mint request sent"))?;
    let secret = read_secret(&store).map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot read the 0600 store token proof; no mint request sent"))?;
    let mint = Mint { service: name.into(), days, store: protocol::store_identity(&store)
        .map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot identify the owned store; no mint request sent"))? };
    let body = serde_json::to_string(&mint).unwrap();
    if body.len() > BODY_LIMIT { return Err((1, "token: E_AUTH at --service: a service name is 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -; use --service worker".into())); }
    let binding = mint.binding();
    let client = protocol::random().map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot create a fresh challenge; no mint request sent"))?;
    let headers = format!("{CLIENT}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&client));
    let challenge = exchange(address, &headers, &body).map_err(|_| unavailable("server challenge unavailable; no mint proof sent"))?;
    let server = challenge["server_nonce"].as_str().and_then(protocol::nonce)
        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("invalid server challenge; no mint proof sent"))?;
    if !challenge["server_proof"].as_str().is_some_and(|proof| protocol::verify_server(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, proof)) {
        return Err(unavailable("server proof did not verify; no mint proof sent"));
    }
    // Only a verified server receives a client proof. Neither message contains the key.
    let headers = format!("{headers}{SERVER}: {}\r\n{PROOF}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&server),
        protocol::client_proof(&secret, &client, &server, &binding));
    let reply = exchange(address, &headers, &body)
        .map_err(|_| unavailable("mint reply unavailable; a session may have been minted, but no token was received"))?;
    let reply: Json = reply["sealed"].as_str().and_then(|sealed| protocol::open(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, sealed))
        .and_then(|bytes| serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).ok())
        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"))?;
    if let Some(denied) = reply.get("denied") {
        if let Ok(why) = serde_json::from_value::<Refusal>(denied.clone()) {
            return Err((1, format!("token: {} at {ENDPOINT}: {}; {}", why.code, why.message, why.rewrite.unwrap_or_else(|| "rerun snapback4 token with valid service arguments".into()))));
        }
        return Err(unavailable("server refused the local mint request; restart it with this CLI and retry"));
    }
    let session = &reply["session"];
    let principal = format!("service:{name}");
    let token = session["token"].as_str().filter(|t| t.len() == 43 && t.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"_-".contains(&c)));
    if session["principal"] != principal || session["kind"] != "service" || token.is_none() || session["expires_at"].as_i64().is_none() {
        return Err(unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"));
    }
    Ok(Session { scope: snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full, cap_expires_at: None, principal, kind: "service".into(), token: token.unwrap().into(), expires_at: session["expires_at"].as_i64().unwrap() })
}

/// Whether a value is (or contains) the reserved system principal.
pub fn contains_system_principal(value: &Value) -> bool {
    match value {
        Value::Id(id) => id == crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL,
        Value::Array(items) => items.iter().any(contains_system_principal),
        Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) => fields.values().any(contains_system_principal),
        _ => false,
    }
}

/// Missing optional columns read as null; missing required columns with a
/// literal default read that value. Contextual defaults belong to inserts.
pub fn materialize_optional_fields(row: &mut Value, table: &Table) {
    let fields = match row {
        Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) => fields,
        _ => return,
    };
    for (name, kind) in &table.columns {
        if !fields.contains_key(name) {
            if table.maintained.contains_key(name) {
                fields.insert(name.clone(), Value::Int(0));
            } else if kind.is_optional() {
                fields.insert(name.clone(), Value::Null);
            } else if let Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(value)) = table.defaults.get(name) {
                fields.insert(name.clone(), value.clone());
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Borrow complete images; materialize old images only when needed.
pub fn row_image<'a>(row: &'a Value, table: &Table) -> std::borrow::Cow<'a, Value> {
    if table.columns.keys().all(|column| row.field(column).is_ok()) { return std::borrow::Cow::Borrowed(row); }
    let mut image = row.clone();
    materialize_optional_fields(&mut image, table);
    std::borrow::Cow::Owned(image)
}

/// The type an argument declares as a column type, for value validation.
pub fn arg_as_column(kind: &ArgType) -> Option<ColumnType> {
    Some(match kind {
        ArgType::Cursor | ArgType::Scalar => return None,
        ArgType::Id => ColumnType::Id,
        ArgType::Principal => ColumnType::Principal,
        ArgType::Ref { table } => ColumnType::Ref { table: table.clone() },
        ArgType::Text { max, .. } => ColumnType::Text { max: *max },
        ArgType::Bytes { max } => ColumnType::Bytes { max: *max },
        ArgType::Image { max } => ColumnType::Image { max: *max, once: false },
        ArgType::Video { max } => ColumnType::Video { max: *max },
        ArgType::File { max, types } => ColumnType::File { max: *max, types: types.clone() },
        ArgType::Int => ColumnType::Int,
        ArgType::IntRange { min, max } => ColumnType::IntRange { min: *min, max: *max },
        ArgType::Bool => ColumnType::Bool,
        ArgType::Time => ColumnType::Time,
        ArgType::Json { max_bytes } => ColumnType::Json { max_bytes: *max_bytes },
        ArgType::Optional(inner) => ColumnType::Optional(Box::new(arg_as_column(inner)?)),
        ArgType::List { of, max } => ColumnType::List { of: Box::new(of.as_deref().map(arg_as_column).flatten().unwrap_or(ColumnType::Json { max_bytes: 4096 })), max: *max },
        ArgType::ListRange { of, min, max } => ColumnType::ListRange { of: Box::new(of.as_deref().and_then(arg_as_column).unwrap_or(ColumnType::Json { max_bytes: 4096 })), min: *min, max: *max },
        ArgType::Enum { variants } => ColumnType::Enum { variants: variants.clone() },
        ArgType::Decimal { scale } => ColumnType::Decimal { scale: *scale },
        ArgType::Money { currency, scale } => ColumnType::Money { currency: currency.clone(), scale: *scale },
    })
}

//! Private filesystem-owner control transport; never an HTTP application door.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — the host remains the only database writer.
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::{Value, json};
#[cfg(unix)]
use std::os::unix::{
    ffi::OsStrExt,
    fs::{DirBuilderExt, FileTypeExt, MetadataExt, OpenOptionsExt, PermissionsExt},
    net::{UnixListener, UnixStream},
};
use std::{
    fs,
    io::{self, Read, Write},
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};

const INPUT: usize = 4096;
const OUTPUT: usize = 64 * 1024;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "command", rename_all = "kebab-case", deny_unknown_fields)]
pub(crate) enum Command {
    Status {
        after: u64,
        items_after: Option<String>,
        event: Option<u64>,
    },
    Activate {
        release: u64,
        expected_release: Option<u64>,
        expected_revision: u64,
        digest: String,
        rollback: bool,
        restore_dormant: bool,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    },
    Prune {
        confirm: bool,
        expected_revision: u64,
    },
}
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct Envelope {
    identity: String,
    command: Command,
}

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(crate) fn identity(store: &Path) -> Result<String, String> {
    let path = store.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let m = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&path)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
        .ok_or("store disappeared")?;
    Ok(crate::native::digest(
        &serde_json::to_vec(&(path.as_os_str().as_bytes(), m.dev(), m.ino())).unwrap(),
    ))
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn peer(stream: &UnixStream) -> io::Result<()> {
    use std::os::fd::AsRawFd;
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    let uid = {
        let mut credentials = std::mem::MaybeUninit::<libc::ucred>::uninit();
        let mut size = std::mem::size_of::<libc::ucred>() as libc::socklen_t;
        if unsafe {
            libc::getsockopt(
                stream.as_raw_fd(),
                libc::SOL_SOCKET,
                libc::SO_PEERCRED,
                credentials.as_mut_ptr().cast(),
                &mut size,
            )
        } != 0
        {
            return Err(io::Error::last_os_error());
        }
        if size as usize != std::mem::size_of::<libc::ucred>() {
            return Err(io::Error::other("invalid peer credentials"));
        }
        unsafe { credentials.assume_init() }.uid
    };
    #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
    let uid = {
        let (mut uid, mut gid) = (0, 0);
        if unsafe { libc::getpeereid(stream.as_raw_fd(), &mut uid, &mut gid) } != 0 {
            return Err(io::Error::last_os_error());
        }
        uid
    };
    #[cfg(not(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "macos")))]
    return Err(io::Error::other(
        "release control peer credentials unsupported on this Unix host",
    ));
    #[cfg(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "macos"))]
    if uid != unsafe { libc::geteuid() } {
        return Err(io::Error::other(
            "release control requires the same filesystem owner",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn socket_entry(path: &Path) -> io::Result<fs::Metadata> {
    let m = fs::symlink_metadata(path)?;
    if !m.file_type().is_socket()
        || m.uid() != unsafe { libc::geteuid() }
        || m.permissions().mode() & 0o7777 != 0o600
    {
        return Err(io::Error::other(
            "release control must be a same-owner 0600 socket, never a symlink or regular file",
        ));
    }
    Ok(m)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#51-path-independent-control-address — fixed short runtime root, independent of shell environment.
#[cfg(unix)]
fn private_directory(path: &Path, create: bool) -> io::Result<()> {
    if create {
        match fs::DirBuilder::new().mode(0o700).create(path) {
            Ok(()) => {}

const fetchIdentity = async (url: string, timeoutMs: number) => {
  const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs) });
  return { response, body: await response.json() as Record<string, unknown> };
};
async function nativeHost(verb: "dev" | "serve", prefix: string[], options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost & { personas?: string[] }> {
  const env = { ...process.env, ...options.env }, id = randomUUID();
  const ports = portRange(env), timeout = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
  const args = [...(options.args ?? [])];
  if (!args.includes("--port")) args.push("--port", ports);
  const run = logged(resolveBinary(), [verb, ...prefix, ...args], { ...options, env: { ...env, SNAPBACK4_PROOF_ID: id, SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID: String(process.pid) } });
  try {
    const url = await ready(run, line => {
      const match = line.match(/^SNAPBACK4_READY verb=(dev|serve) generation=\d+ url=(http:\/\/[^ ]+) pid=(\d+)(?: |$)/);
      if (!match) return;
      if (match[1] !== verb || Number(match[3]) !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof ready identity mismatch");
      return match[2];
    }, timeout);
    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/auth/config`, timeout);
    if (!response.ok || body.proof_id !== id) throw Error("proof served identity mismatch");
    let personas: string[] = [];
    if (verb === "dev" && body.devDoors !== false) {
      const status = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/dev/status`, timeout);
      if (status.body.pid !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof dev PID mismatch");
      if (!Array.isArray(status.body.personas) || !status.body.personas.every(name => typeof name === "string")) throw Error("proof dev personas missing");
      personas = status.body.personas;
    }
    return { url, ...(verb === "dev" ? { personas } : {}), pid: run.child.pid!, logs: run.logs, stop: () => stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)) };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
}
export interface DevHost extends ProofHost {
  personas: string[];
  /** Read the current authoritative development time, including all clock advances. */
  now(): Promise<number>;
}
/** The array form preserves the former startDev(args) recipe. */
/** @throws invalid port range or conflicting memory flags; child exits, readiness deadline, PID/served identity mismatch, or unavailable dev clock. */
export function startDev(options: ProofOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<DevHost> {
  const opts: ProofOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ProofOptions;
  const args = [...(opts.args ?? [])];
  const persistent = args.some(arg => arg === "--store" || arg.startsWith("--store=") || arg === "--no-memory");
  if (persistent && args.includes("--memory")) throw new TypeError("proof startDev: --memory conflicts with --store/--no-memory");
  // Native persistence is the default. --no-memory remains a consumed explicit alias.
  const host = nativeHost("dev", [], { ...opts, args: args.filter(arg => arg !== "--no-memory") });
  return host.then(host => ({ ...host, personas: host.personas!, now: async () => {
    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${host.url}/dev/status`, deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 10_000));
    if (!response.ok || typeof body.now !== "number") throw Error("proof dev clock unavailable; password-only dev requires explicit --unsafe-dev-doors for clock/fixture tools; use serve staging for real users");
