use super::*;

/// Who is asking: a bearer session, or (in `dev`, from loopback) a persona.
pub(crate) fn principal_of(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<snapback4_core::identity::Capability, Refusal> {
    principal_with_owner(request, shared, None)
}

fn principal_with_owner(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, owner: Option<&Engine>) -> Result<snapback4_core::identity::Capability, Refusal> {
    // Declared public bearer routes keep their existing development admission.
    if !(request.method() == &Method::Post && routes::declared(shared, request.url()).is_some()
        && !request.headers().iter().any(|h| h.field.equiv("x-snapback-persona"))) {
        dev_request(request, shared)?;
    }
    let mut persona = None;
    let mut bearer = None;
    for header in request.headers() {
        let name = header.field.as_str().as_str().to_ascii_lowercase();
        let value = header.value.as_str().trim();
        if name == "x-snapback-persona" {
            if persona.is_some()
                || value.is_empty()
                || !value.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_' || c == '-')
            {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send exactly one valid x-snapback-persona header").guide("personas"));
            }
            persona = Some(value.to_owned());
        }
        if name == "authorization" {
            let Some(token) = crate::auth::parse_bearer(value) else {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send Authorization: Bearer <token>").guide("personas"));
            };
            if bearer.replace(token.to_owned()).is_some() {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send exactly one Authorization header").guide("personas"));
            }
        }
    }
    if persona.is_some() && bearer.is_some() {
        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send one credential: a development persona or a bearer token").guide("personas"));
    }
    if let Some(persona) = persona {
        if let Some(engine) = owner { shared.dev_authority(engine)?; }
        if shared.dev_doors() {
            // `use personas` is the cast: a test expecting an outsider to be
            // refused must not see it signed in. No declaration admits none.
            // (Taken briefly; the engine
            // guard, when held, is always acquired before this one.)
            let declared = lock(&shared.bound).personas.clone();
            if !declared.contains(&persona) {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", format!(
                    "persona {persona} is not declared; `use personas {}` is this project's cast. Add {persona} to it (dev re-adopts on save) or send a declared persona", declared.join(", ")))
                    .rewrite(format!("use personas {}{persona}", if declared.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!("{}, ", declared.join(", ")) })).guide("personas"));
            }
            return Ok((persona_principal(&persona), "persona".into(), snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full));
        }
        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "personas are a development door; a deployed server takes bearer tokens (snapback4/auth)").guide("personas"));
    }
    if let Some(token) = bearer {
        // Reads may authenticate against the committed generation during adoption.
        // Keep write authentication behind the writer: time queued for adoption
        // must not preserve a session that expired or was revoked in that wait.
        let path = request.url().split('?').next().unwrap_or("");
        let read = detached_request(request.method(), path);
        let resolve = |engine: &Engine, identity: Option<&str>, detached: bool| {
            let now = shared.now();
            let sample = snapback4_core::identity::ServiceSample { now, supervisor_revision: shared.worker_revision.load(Ordering::Acquire) };
            let result = if detached {
                snapback4_core::identity::resolve_read_only_supervised(engine.store(), &token, sample, identity)
            } else {
                snapback4_core::identity::resolve_supervised(engine.store(), &token, sample)
            };
            let result=result.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|value|value.ok_or_else(||
                snapback4_core::identity::diagnostics::refused(engine.store(), &token, now, trusted_dev_request(request, shared))));
            if result.is_err() {crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());}
            result
        };
        return if let Some(engine) = owner { resolve(engine, None, false) }
        else if read { match shared.reader() {
            Ok((epoch, reader)) => {
                let identity = lock(&shared.wake).store_id.clone();
                let result = resolve(&reader, identity.as_deref(), true);
                lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
                result
            }
            Err(_) if shared.memory_store() => resolve(&lock(&shared.engine), None, false),
            Err(error) => Err(error.refusal()),
        } } else { resolve(&lock(&shared.engine), None, false) };
    }
    Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "sign in: send a bearer token (snapback4/auth), or x-snapback-persona in development").guide("personas"))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#4-development-doors — a persona cannot cross from authentication into a replacement password-only reader.
pub(crate) fn persona_read_authority(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, engine: &Engine) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    let Some(persona) = request.headers().iter().find(|header| header.field.equiv("x-snapback-persona")) else { return Ok(()) };
    shared.dev_authority(engine)?;
    let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
    if (!shared.unsafe_dev_doors && bound.schema.auth.password && !bound.schema.auth.guests)
        || !bound.personas.iter().any(|name| name == persona.value.as_str().trim()) {
        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "persona authority changed while this read waited; sign in with a password or use the current declared development cast").guide("staging"));
    }
    // The cast is checked at this read's admission. Never hold its publication
    // mutex during I/O: concurrent ordinary writes still need to authenticate.
    // The engine transaction fences a replaced execution schema.
    Ok(())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — adoption's writer queue cannot retain expired bearer authority.
pub(crate) fn mutation_authority(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, engine: &Engine, principal: &str) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    if principal_with_owner(request, shared, Some(engine))?.0 == principal { Ok(()) }
    else { Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "the request's session changed; sign in again").guide("personas")) }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — additions require an explicit decision here.
pub(super) fn read_permitted(method: &Method, path: &str) -> bool {
    match method {
        Method::Get => matches!(path, "/changes" | "/probe" | "/schema") || path.starts_with("/assets/"),
        Method::Post => matches!(path, "/sync" | "/probe" | "/auth/refresh" | "/auth/logout") || path.starts_with("/q/"),
        _ => false,
    }
}

                    dir.display()
                );
            }
            Ok(())
        })(),
    )
}

pub fn serve_release(options: &Options) -> Result<(), String> {
    let requested_port = crate::port_flag::parse("serve", options.flags.get("port").map(String::as_str))?;
    let bind = release_bind(options)?;
    let capacity = crate::host_limits::Capacity::from_options(&options.flags, |name| {
        std::env::var(name).ok()
    })?;
    let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
    crate::host_limits::initialize();
    let owner = crate::store_owner::host(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))?;
    owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
    let clock_offset = options.flags.get("clock-offset").map(|text| crate::dev_clock::offset(text).map_err(|e| e.to_string())).transpose()?.unwrap_or(0);
    if options.flags.contains_key("clock-offset") {
        let store = owner.open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5)).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if store.meta("deployment_environment").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release().to_string()); }
    }
    let current = current(&dir, &owner)?.ok_or("no current release; deploy first")?;
    let release = Project {
        root: dir.join("releases").join(current.to_string())
            .canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    };
    crate::release_files::link_store(&release.root)?;
    let (port, listener) = crate::port_flag::listen("serve", requested_port, bind, None)?;
    let worker = options
        .flags
        .get("worker")
        .map(|path| {
            Path::new(path)
                .canonicalize()
                .map_err(|e| format!("--worker {path}: {e}"))
        })
        .transpose()?;
    crate::serve::serve(
        release,
        port,
        crate::serve::Mode::Release {
            clock_offset,
            expected: current,
            worker,
            owner: std::sync::Arc::new(owner),
        },
        &bind.to_string(),
        listener,
        capacity,
    )
}

pub(crate) fn release_bind(options: &Options) -> Result<std::net::IpAddr, String> {
    let bind = options
        .flags
        .get("bind")
        .map(String::as_str)
        .unwrap_or("127.0.0.1");
    let address: std::net::IpAddr = bind
        .parse()
        .map_err(|_| "--bind must be a literal IPv4 or IPv6 address".to_owned())?;
    if !address.is_loopback() {
        if !options.flags.contains_key("i-understand-cleartext") {
            return Err("non-loopback HTTP sends passwords and bearer tokens in cleartext; use a loopback listener behind a TLS proxy, or explicitly pass --i-understand-cleartext".into());
        }
        eprintln!(
            "WARNING: listening on {address} over CLEARTEXT HTTP. Passwords, bearer tokens, schema and data have no transport protection. Expose only through a TLS proxy on a trusted private hop; see snapback4/README.md."
        );

//! One wire fence for engine semantics an older decoder must not ignore.
//! Constraint is strictly parsed by 0.2.33, unlike Schema's optional fields.
use super::*;
use serde::{Deserialize, Deserializer, Serialize, Serializer};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

// Private remote derive: the schema has one wire field list, and callers
// cannot accidentally invoke an unchecked inherent Schema::deserialize.
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(remote = "Schema")]
struct Wire {
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    backfills: Vec<backfill::Backfill>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
    receipts: Vec<ReceiptFamily>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BTreeMap::is_empty")]
    receipt_dependencies: BTreeMap<String, BTreeSet<String>>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BTreeMap::is_empty")]
    source_reservations: BTreeMap<String, reservations::WriteReservation>,
    #[serde(skip)]
    legacy_receipt_prediction: bool,
    #[serde(skip)]
    legacy_semantic_capabilities: LegacySemanticCapabilities,
    tables: BTreeMap<String, Table>,
    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    #[serde(skip)]
    authored_indexes: inspection::AuthoredIndexes,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
    delivery: Vec<DeliveryBinding>,
    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    #[serde(skip)]
    delivery_lookup: crate::engine::delivery::Lookup,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
    timelines: Vec<timeline::Timeline>,
    #[serde(default)]
    maintains: Vec<Maintain>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Auth::is_default")]
    auth: Auth,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
    effects: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
    schedules: Vec<Schedule>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BTreeMap::is_empty")]
    operations: BTreeMap<String, Execution>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BTreeMap::is_empty")]
    unique_refusals: BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, UniqueRefusal>>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BTreeMap::is_empty")]
    action_specs: BTreeMap<String, ActionSpec>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BTreeMap::is_empty")]
    effect_specs: BTreeMap<String, WorkSpec>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BTreeMap::is_empty")]
    routes: BTreeMap<String, RouteSpec>,
}

// Add a capability and its schema predicate here in one line. This is both
// the derivation and the decoder's allowlist; authors never declare it.
const CAPABILITIES: &[(&str, fn(&Schema) -> bool)] = &[
    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    ("release-backfills-v3", |s| !s.backfills.is_empty()),
    ("live-parent-copy-v1", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.live.is_some()))),
    ("source-write-reservations-v2", |s| s.source_reservations.values().any(|p| p.birth.is_some())),
    ("source-write-reservations-v1", |s| !s.source_reservations.is_empty()),
    // @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — older devices must not execute newly eligible
    // writes using their receipt-derived maintenance path.

        || s.has_receipt_columns()),
    ("rule-reference-v2", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t|

        || t.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert { key, .. } if key.iter().any(|term| term.reference_source().is_some()))))),
    ("rule-reference", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| [&t.rules.insert, &t.rules.update, &t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r| r.has_reference()))),
    ("declarative-receipts-v4", |s| !s.receipts.is_empty()),
    ("owner-key-coverage", |s| s.maintains.iter().any(|m| m.own_keys.is_some())),
    ("ephemeral", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| t.ephemeral.is_some())),
    ("field-authority", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| !t.written_by.is_empty() || t.update_transition)),
    // @ref LLP 3000.036#11 — an older decoder refuses ordered insert rules rather than misjudging them.
    ("insert-rule-order", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| ordered(&t.rules.insert))),

    ("reference-actions", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| !t.on_delete.is_empty())),

    // through actions nor queue an unproved same-id physical predecessor.

//! Server construction facts belong to the transaction that accepts the open.
// @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — no fallible construction reads after commit.
use super::*;

pub(crate) struct Metadata {
    pub store_id: String,
    pub dev_effects: bool,
}

impl Metadata {
    pub fn read(tx: &Tx<'_>) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Ok(Self {
            store_id: tx.meta("store_id")?.unwrap_or_default(),
            dev_effects: tx.meta("release")?.is_none(),
        })
    }
}

/// An open that needs activation releases its first snapshot. Bind that repair
/// to the exact stored backend it validated, not just the declared hash.
pub(crate) struct Backend(pub i64, pub String, pub String);

impl Backend {
    pub fn check(&self, conn: &Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let held: (i64, String, String) = conn.query_row(
            "SELECT g.generation, g.schema, g.programs FROM _generations g JOIN _meta m ON m.key = 'generation' AND g.generation = CAST(m.value AS INTEGER)",
            [], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?, row.get(2)?)))?;
        if held.0 != self.0 || held.1 != self.1 || held.2 != self.2 {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("the stored backend changed while opening; retry open".into()));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Store {
    pub(super) fn check_version(conn: &Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let version: String = conn.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'version'", [], |r| r.get(0))?;
        if version != STORE_VERSION && version != super::dormant::VERSION && version != "snapback4-release-store-2" && version != super::backfills::VERSION {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Store(format!("store version {version} is not {STORE_VERSION}; this data directory belongs to another Snapback")));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub(crate) fn opening_transaction(&mut self) -> Result<Tx<'_>, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = if self.conn.is_readonly("main")? { self.read_transaction()? } else { self.transaction()? };
        Self::check_version(tx.conn())?;
        let catalog = tx.conn().prepare("SELECT name, num FROM _catalog")?.query_map([], |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<BTreeMap<String, i64>, _>>()?;
        if &catalog != tx.catalog {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("the catalog changed while opening; retry open".into()));
        }
        Ok(tx)
    }

    /// The caller keeps its validation transaction until all construction reads
    /// and upgrades finish. Read-only inspection never installs anything.
    pub(crate) fn finish_open(tx: Tx<'_>) -> Result<Metadata, RuntimeError> {
        if tx.conn().is_readonly("main")? {
            let metadata = Metadata::read(&tx)?;
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(metadata);
        }
        Self::initialize_schema(tx.conn())?;
        Self::initialize_change_evidence(tx.conn())?;
        let metadata = Metadata::read(&tx)?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(metadata)
    }
}

//! The runtime meter (LLP 2000.000 §6): resources are charged as a program
//! runs, deterministically (counts, never time), on the server and on a
//! device alike. A query cut by the meter returns `capped`; a mutation
//! aborts with `E_BOUND` naming the actual work.

use crate::error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// The ceilings. They keep Snapback 2's names and roughly its values.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Ceilings {
    pub examined: u64,
    pub index_entries: u64,
    pub rule_probes: u64,
    pub steps: u64,
    pub heap_bytes: u64,
    pub output_bytes: u64,
    pub rows_written: u64,
}

impl Default for Ceilings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self { examined: 8_192, index_entries: 16_384, rule_probes: 8_192, steps: 1_000_000, heap_bytes: 16 << 20, output_bytes: 1 << 20, rows_written: 1_024 }
    }
}

impl Ceilings {
    /// A job runs one chunk at a time and may write more.
    pub fn job() -> Self {
        Self { rows_written: 8_192, examined: 16_384, ..Self::default() }
    }

    /// The engine's own partition maintenance is bounded by audience, not by
    /// the author's ceilings.
    pub fn unbounded() -> Self {
        Self { examined: u64::MAX, index_entries: u64::MAX, rule_probes: u64::MAX, steps: u64::MAX, heap_bytes: u64::MAX, output_bytes: u64::MAX, rows_written: u64::MAX }
    }

    fn of(&self, kind: MeterKind) -> u64 {
        match kind {
            MeterKind::Examined => self.examined,
            MeterKind::IndexEntries => self.index_entries,
            MeterKind::RuleProbes => self.rule_probes,
            MeterKind::Steps => self.steps,
            MeterKind::HeapBytes => self.heap_bytes,
